    const rebuiltStepsOf = (rows: readonly RebuiltRow[], lanesFrom: Schedule): RebuiltStep[] =>
      rows.map(([id, startMin, endMin, slackMin, waitMin]) => ({
        id,
        startMin,
        endMin,
        lanes: lanesFrom.steps.find((x) => x.id === id)?.lanes ?? {
          burner: null,
          oven: null,
          cook: null,
        },
        slackMin,
        critical: false,
        waitMin,
      }));
    const ivOf = (rows: readonly { id: string; startMin: number; endMin: number }[]): Iv =>
      Object.fromEntries(rows.map((r) => [r.id, { startMin: r.startMin, endMin: r.endMin }]));

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat >> test/meta/oracles.test.ts <<'EOF'

describe('R7 oracleLatestPosition with the serving minute and the pinned intervals, on RB-a', () => {
  // RB-a: servingMin 15, roast running [-100,-10), prep and shred done; the seven pending steps
  // are all hot (they have a hold). The oracle is told where serving is and which intervals are
  // pinned; a step's deadline is the serving minute or the earliest start of a pending user.
  const finalIv = ivOf(rebuiltStepsOf(RB_A_ROWS, H1_SCHEDULE));
  const pinned = oraclePinnedIntervals(SUNDAY_ROAST, RB_A_PROGRESS, RB_A_NOW);
  const startsFrom = (iv: Iv): Record<string, number> =>
    Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(iv).map(([id, i]) => [id, i.startMin]));
  const context = { servingMin: 15, pinned };
  const pendingIds = [
    'chicken.carve',
    'chicken.rest',
    'gravy.roux',
    'gravy.simmer',
    'gravy.strain',
    'greens.boil',
    'greens.toss',
  ];

  it('every pending step of the hand-worked RB-a sits at its latest valid position', () => {
    const starts = startsFrom(finalIv);
    expect(
      pendingIds.map((id) => [
        id,
        oracleLatestPosition(SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, starts, id, context),
      ]),
    ).toEqual(pendingIds.map((id) => [id, starts[id]]));
  });

  it('gravy.strain at its forward-repair start 2 can still move to 8: the cook is busy with toss at 7 and with carve from 10, and it has no user before serving 15', () => {
    const starts = { ...startsFrom(finalIv), 'gravy.strain': 2 };
    expect(oracleLatestPosition(SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, starts, 'gravy.strain', context)).toBe(
      8,
    );
  });

  it('gravy.roux at its forward-repair start -10 can move to -4: gravy.simmer, its pending user, starts at -2 and nothing holds the cook or a burner in -4..-2', () => {
    const starts = { ...startsFrom(finalIv), 'gravy.roux': -10 };
    expect(oracleLatestPosition(SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, starts, 'gravy.roux', context)).toBe(
      -4,
    );
  });

  it('without a context the serving minute is 0, as for G9: the same strain cannot move', () => {
    const starts = { ...startsFrom(finalIv), 'gravy.strain': 2 };
    expect(oracleLatestPosition(SUNDAY_ROAST, HOME_KITCHEN, starts, 'gravy.strain')).toBe(2);
  });
});
EOF

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='test/support/oracles.ts'
s=open(p).read()
start=s.index("/**\n * G9: the latest start the step can have")
end=s.index("export function startsOf")
new='''/**
 * For a rebuilt schedule (R7): where serving is, and the intervals of the pinned steps (done
 * and running), which are the ones `startsOf`-style start maps cannot describe.
 */
export interface LatestPositionContext {
  servingMin: number;
  pinned: Iv;
}

/**
 * G9: the latest start the step can have with everything else fixed (lanes ignored). Tries every
 * delay d in 1..(deadline - end), where the deadline is minute 0 or the earliest start of a
 * user, and keeps the largest d for which the step's resources are free in all its new minutes
 * (G1; G2 and G3 hold by the deadline). Returns the current start when no delay works.
 *
 * R7: with a `context`, the deadline starts at `context.servingMin`, only users that are not
 * pinned bound it (a pinned user has already happened), and a pinned step occupies its pinned
 * interval rather than `[start, start + minutes)`.
 */
export function oracleLatestPosition(
  plan: Plan,
  kitchen: Kitchen,
  starts: Starts,
  stepId: string,
  context?: LatestPositionContext,
): number {
  const step = plan.steps.find((s) => s.id === stepId);
  if (step === undefined) return 0;
  const current = startOf(starts, step);
  const pinnedIv = context?.pinned ?? {};
  const intervalFor = (o: PlanStep): { startMin: number; endMin: number } =>
    pinnedIv[o.id] ?? { startMin: startOf(starts, o), endMin: endOf(starts, o) };
  const users = usersOf(plan, stepId).filter((u) => pinnedIv[u.id] === undefined);
  let deadline = context?.servingMin ?? 0;
  for (const u of users) deadline = Math.min(deadline, startOf(starts, u));
  // Minutes in use by every other step, per resource this step needs (G1 for the new place).
  const used = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const o of plan.steps) {
    if (o.id === stepId) continue;
    const { startMin, endMin } = intervalFor(o);
    for (const resource of RESOURCE_NAMES) {
      if (!usesResource(step, resource) || !usesResource(o, resource)) continue;
      for (let minute = startMin; minute < endMin; minute++) {
        const key = `${resource}:${minute}`;
        used.set(key, (used.get(key) ?? 0) + 1);
      }
    }
  }
  let latest = current;
  for (let d = 1; current + d + step.minutes <= deadline; d++) {
    let fits = true;
    for (let minute = current + d; minute < current + d + step.minutes && fits; minute++) {
      for (const resource of RESOURCE_NAMES) {
        if (!usesResource(step, resource)) continue;
        if ((used.get(`${resource}:${minute}`) ?? 0) + 1 > limitOf(kitchen, resource)) fits = false;
      }
    }
    if (fits) latest = current + d;
  }
  return latest;
}

'''
s=s[:start]+new+s[end:]
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

    … (77 earlier lines not shown)
    const familyOf = (id: string): string => id.charAt(0);
    
    const TEST_OPEN = /(?<![\w.$])(?:it|test)\(\s*(['"`])/g;
    
    /**
     * The first argument of every `it(` or `test(` call: a quoted or template literal, cut at its
     * closing quote or, in a template, at the first `${`. `it.each` and `.test(` are not matched.
     */
    function extractTitles(source: string): string[] {
      const titles: string[] = [];
      for (const match of source.matchAll(TEST_OPEN)) {
        const quote = match[1] ?? "'";
        let i = (match.index ?? 0) + match[0].length;
        let title = '';
        while (i < source.length) {
          const ch = source.charAt(i);
          if (ch === '\\') {
            title += source.charAt(i + 1);
            i += 2;
            continue;
          }
          if (ch === quote) break;
          if (quote === '`' && ch === '$' && source.charAt(i + 1) === '{') break;
          title += ch;
          i++;
        }
        titles.push(title);
      }
      return titles;
    }
    exit 0

describe('R5.1 ServeTimeSchema: a 24-hour time HH:MM from 00:00 to 23:59', () => {
  it.each(['00:00', '09:05', '19:00', '23:59', '12:30', '20:59'])('accepts %s', (value) => {
    expect(ServeTimeSchema.safeParse(value).success).toBe(true);
  });
  it.each([
    ['24:00', 'hour 24'],
    ['7:00', 'one-digit hour'],
    ['19:60', 'minute 60'],
    ['19:00 ', 'trailing space'],
    [' 19:00', 'leading space'],
    ['19:00\\n', 'trailing line feed'],
    ['1900', 'no colon'],
    ['', 'empty'],
    ['19:5', 'one-digit minute'],
    ['29:00', 'hour 29'],
    ['19:00:00', 'seconds'],
  ])('rejects %j (%s)', (value) => {
    expect(ServeTimeSchema.safeParse(value).success).toBe(false);
  });
  it('rejects values that are not strings', () => {
    expect(ServeTimeSchema.safeParse(1900).success).toBe(false);
    expect(ServeTimeSchema.safeParse(null).success).toBe(false);
  });
  it('SERVE_TIME_PATTERN is anchored: it matches 19:00 and not x19:00', () => {
    expect(SERVE_TIME_PATTERN.test('19:00')).toBe(true);
    expect(SERVE_TIME_PATTERN.test('x19:00')).toBe(false);
  });
  it('ServeTime is a string', () => {
    expectTypeOf<z.infer<typeof ServeTimeSchema>>().toEqualTypeOf<string>();
  });
});
'''
open(p,'w').write(s)

cat > src/core/dinnerfile.ts <<'EOF'
import { err } from './result.js';
import type { Result } from './result.js';
import type { Issue, Kitchen, Plan, ServeTime } from './schema.js';

export interface DinnerfileOptions {
  kitchen?: Kitchen;
  serve?: ServeTime;
}

export interface DinnerfileImport {
  /** Lexically checked only (R6.4); the caller runs validatePlan on it. Canonical order. */
  plan: Plan;
  /** Keys present only when their line is present. */
  options: DinnerfileOptions;
}

export type MetadataKeyword = 'plan' | 'dish' | 'step' | 'kitchen' | 'serve';

export type DinnerfileError =
  | {
      code: 'unsupported_line';
      line: number;
      message: string;
      reason: 'space_before_colon' | 'double_colon' | 'several_targets' | 'other';
    }
  | { code: 'line_continuation'; line: number; message: string }
  | { code: 'recipe_before_rule'; line: number; message: string }
  | { code: 'bad_target'; line: number; message: string }
  | {
      code: 'bad_prerequisite';
      line: number;
      message: string;
      stepId?: string;
      dishId?: string;
    }
  | { code: 'missing_plan'; line: null; message: string }
  | {
      code: 'duplicate_metadata';
      line: number;
      message: string;
      keyword: 'plan' | 'kitchen' | 'serve';
      firstLine: number;
    }
  | { code: 'bad_metadata'; line: number; message: string; keyword: MetadataKeyword }
  | {
      code: 'unknown_key';
      line: number;
      message: string;
      keyword: 'step' | 'kitchen';
      stepId?: string;
    }
  | {
      code: 'duplicate_key';
      line: number;
      message: string;
      keyword: 'step' | 'kitchen';
      key: string;
      stepId?: string;
    }
  | {
      code: 'missing_key';
      line: number;
      message: string;
      keyword: 'step' | 'kitchen';
      key: string;
      stepId?: string;
    }
  | {
      code: 'bad_value';
      line: number;
      message: string;
      keyword: MetadataKeyword;
      key: string;
      stepId?: string;
    }
  | { code: 'step_without_target'; line: number; message: string }
  | { code: 'target_without_step'; line: number; message: string; stepId: string }
  | {
      code: 'duplicate_target';
      line: number;
      message: string;
      target: string;
      firstLine: number;
      stepId?: string;
      dishId?: string;
    }
  | { code: 'dish_target_mismatch'; line: number; message: string; dishId: string }
  | { code: 'dinner_mismatch'; line: number; message: string };

export type DinnerfileErrorCode = DinnerfileError['code'];

/** The Dinnerfile text of a plan. err when the plan or the options fail their schemas. */
export function toDinnerfile(_plan: Plan, _options?: DinnerfileOptions): Result<string, Issue[]> {
  return err([]);
}

/** Reads a Dinnerfile back. Never throws. */
export function fromDinnerfile(_text: string): Result<DinnerfileImport, DinnerfileError[]> {
  return err([]);
}
EOF

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Dinnerfile spec, T1.2: the hostile extensions of the existing generators and the new ones.

/** The hostile alphabet of the dinnerfile design, written out here (not read from the generator). */
const HOSTILE_TOKENS: readonly string[] = [
  "'",
  '"',
  '$',
  '$$',
  '$(MAKE)',
  '${MAKE}',
  '$(shell touch canary)',
  '`touch canary`',
  "'\\\\''",
  '; touch canary',
  '#',
  '# step dish=x',
  '\\\\',
  '\\\\c',
  '\\n',
  '\\r',
  '\\r\\n',
  '\\t',
  '\\t@touch canary',
  ':',
  '=',
  ':=',
  '%',
  '|',
  '*',
  '-n',
  '-e',
  '  ',
  '\\u0000',
  '\\u001b[31m',
  '\\u007f',
  '\\u0085',
  '\\u00a0',
  '\\u2028',
  '\\u2029',
  '\\u202e',
  '\\u2066',
  '\\ufeff',
  '\\u00e9',
  'e\\u0301',
  '\\u{1F525}',
  '\\u6f22\\u5b57',
  'word',
];
const LONE_HIGH = /[\\ud800-\\udbff](?![\\udc00-\\udfff])/;
const LONE_LOW = /(?<![\\ud800-\\udbff])[\\udc00-\\udfff]/;

/** Every title, detail, dish name and plan title of the plans, one string each. */
const textsOf = (plans: readonly Plan[]): string[] =>
  plans.flatMap((p) => [
    p.title,
    ...p.dishes.map((d) => d.name),
    ...p.steps.flatMap((s) => (s.detail === undefined ? [s.title] : [s.title, s.detail])),
  ]);

describe('R10.1 (dinnerfile) the extended plan generators stay valid and reach the hostile cases', () => {
  const plans = [
    ...sampleOf(arbPlan(), 400),
    ...fc.sample(arbPlan({ large: true }), { numRuns: 100, seed: SEED }),
  ];
  const texts = textsOf(plans);

  it('every sample, large ones included, passes PlanSchema with unique ids', () => {
    for (const plan of plans) {
      expect(PlanSchema.safeParse(plan).success).toBe(true);
      expect(duplicates(plan.steps.map((s) => s.id))).toEqual([]);
      expect(duplicates(plan.dishes.map((d) => d.id))).toEqual([]);
    }
  });
  it('no sample has a sanity error', () => {
    for (const plan of plans) {
      expect(checkPlan(plan).filter((i) => i.severity === 'error')).toEqual([]);
    }
  });
  it.each(HOSTILE_TOKENS.map((t) => [JSON.stringify(t), t] as const))(
    'some title, name or detail holds the token %s',
    (_name, token) => {
      expect(texts.some((t) => t.includes(token))).toBe(true);
    },
  );
  it('some text holds a lone high surrogate and some a lone low surrogate', () => {
    expect(texts.some((t) => LONE_HIGH.test(t))).toBe(true);
    expect(texts.some((t) => LONE_LOW.test(t))).toBe(true);
  });
  it('some plan has a dish called dinner and one for each directive word', () => {
    const dishIds = new Set(plans.flatMap((p) => p.dishes.map((d) => d.id)));
    for (const id of [
      'dinner',
      'define',
      'export',
      'include',
      'ifeq',
      'else',
      'endif',
      'override',
      'vpath',
      'makefile',
    ]) {
      expect(dishIds.has(id)).toBe(true);
    }
  });
  it('some step slug starts with a dash, some ends with one, and some are only dashes', () => {
    const slugs = plans.flatMap((p) => p.steps.map((s) => s.id.slice(s.dish.length + 1)));
    expect(slugs.some((s) => s.startsWith('-'))).toBe(true);
    expect(slugs.some((s) => s.endsWith('-'))).toBe(true);
    expect(slugs.some((s) => /^-+$/.test(s))).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('R10.1 (dinnerfile) arbHostileText', () => {
  const samples = sampleOf(arbHostileText(120), 300);

  it('gives at most max code points and a non-white-space character', () => {
    for (const text of samples) {
      expect([...text].length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(120);
      expect(/\\S/.test(text)).toBe(true);
    }
    for (const text of sampleOf(arbHostileText(5), 300)) {
      expect([...text].length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(5);
      expect(/\\S/.test(text)).toBe(true);
    }
  });
  it('reaches every token of the hostile alphabet', () => {
    for (const token of HOSTILE_TOKENS) {
      expect(samples.some((t) => t.includes(token))).toBe(true);
    }
  });
});

describe('R10.1 (dinnerfile) arbServeTime and the option generators', () => {
  it('arbServeTime always matches SERVE_TIME_PATTERN and reaches 00:xx, 23:xx and xx:59', () => {
    const times = sampleOf(arbServeTime(), 1000);
    for (const t of times) expect(SERVE_TIME_PATTERN.test(t)).toBe(true);
    expect(times.some((t) => t.startsWith('00:'))).toBe(true);
    expect(times.some((t) => t.startsWith('23:'))).toBe(true);
    expect(times.some((t) => t.endsWith(':59'))).toBe(true);
    expect(times.some((t) => t.endsWith(':00'))).toBe(true);
  });
  it('arbDinnerfileOptions: valid values, every key combination, keys absent never undefined', () => {
    const options = sampleOf(arbDinnerfileOptions(), 300);
    for (const o of options) {
      expect(Object.keys(o).filter((k) => k !== 'kitchen' && k !== 'serve')).toEqual([]);
      if ('kitchen' in o) expect(KitchenSchema.safeParse(o.kitchen).success).toBe(true);
      if ('serve' in o) expect(SERVE_TIME_PATTERN.test(o.serve ?? '')).toBe(true);
    }
    const shapes = new Set(options.map((o) => `${'kitchen' in o}/${'serve' in o}`));
    expect([...shapes].sort()).toEqual(['false/false', 'false/true', 'true/false', 'true/true']);
  });
  it('arbAnyDinnerfileOptions: valid and invalid kitchens and serving times, keys never undefined', () => {
    const options = sampleOf(arbAnyDinnerfileOptions(), 500);
    for (const o of options) {
      expect(Object.keys(o).filter((k) => k !== 'kitchen' && k !== 'serve')).toEqual([]);
      for (const key of ['kitchen', 'serve'] as const) {
        if (key in o) expect(o[key]).not.toBeUndefined();
      }
    }
    const kitchens = options.flatMap((o) => ('kitchen' in o ? [o.kitchen] : []));
    const serves = options.flatMap((o) => ('serve' in o ? [o.serve] : []));
    expect(kitchens.some((k) => KitchenSchema.safeParse(k).success)).toBe(true);
    expect(kitchens.some((k) => !KitchenSchema.safeParse(k).success)).toBe(true);
    expect(serves.some((v) => SERVE_TIME_PATTERN.test(String(v)))).toBe(true);
    expect(serves.some((v) => !SERVE_TIME_PATTERN.test(String(v)))).toBe(true);
    // The named invalid values of the design are all reached.
    for (const bad of ['24:00', '7:00', '19:60', '19:00\\n.PHONY: x', '']) {
      expect(serves).toContain(bad);
    }
    expect(kitchens.some((k) => (k as { burners?: unknown }).burners === -1)).toBe(true);
    expect(kitchens.some((k) => (k as { burners?: unknown }).burners === 9)).toBe(true);
    expect(kitchens.some((k) => (k as { cooks?: unknown }).cooks === 1.5)).toBe(true);
    expect(kitchens.some((k) => typeof k === 'string')).toBe(true);
  });
});
'''
open(p,'w').write(s)

// Dinnerfile generators (dinnerfile spec).

/** The hostile alphabet of the dinnerfile design: what titles and details may hold. */
const HOSTILE_TOKENS: readonly string[] = [
  "'",
  '"',
  '$',
  '$$',
  '$(MAKE)',
  '${MAKE}',
  '$(shell touch canary)',
  '`touch canary`',
  "'\\\\''",
  '; touch canary',
  '#',
  '# step dish=x',
  '\\\\',
  '\\\\c',
  '\\n',
  '\\r',
  '\\r\\n',
  '\\t',
  '\\t@touch canary',
  ':',
  '=',
  ':=',
  '%',
  '|',
  '*',
  '-n',
  '-e',
  ' ',
  '  ',
  '\\u0000',
  '\\u001b[31m',
  '\\u007f',
  '\\u0085',
  '\\u00a0',
  '\\u2028',
  '\\u2029',
  '\\u202e',
  '\\u2066',
  '\\ufeff',
  '\\u00e9',
  'e\\u0301',
  '\\u{1F525}',
  '\\ud800',
  '\\udfff',
  '\\u6f22\\u5b57',
  'word',
];

/**
 * 1 to 12 hostile tokens joined, cut to at most `max` code points, with a non-white-space
 * character. The tokens are the design's alphabet: shell and Make metacharacters, line breaks,
 * control, bidirectional and lone-surrogate code points, accents and emoji.
 */
export function arbHostileText(max: number): fc.Arbitrary<string> {
  return fc
    .array(fc.constantFrom(...HOSTILE_TOKENS), { minLength: 1, maxLength: 12 })
    .map((tokens) => [...tokens.join('')].slice(0, max).join(''))
    .filter((text) => /\\S/.test(text));
}

/** A serving time `HH:MM`, hour 0..23 and minute 0..59, zero-padded. */
export function arbServeTime(): fc.Arbitrary<string> {
  return fc
    .tuple(fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 23 }), fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 59 }))
    .map(([h, m]) => `${String(h).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}`);
}

/** Options with a valid kitchen and serving time; each key present or absent, never `undefined`. */
export function arbDinnerfileOptions(): fc.Arbitrary<DinnerfileOptions> {
  return fc.record({ kitchen: arbKitchen(), serve: arbServeTime() }, { requiredKeys: [] });
}

const arbBadKitchen: fc.Arbitrary<unknown> = fc.oneof(
  fc.record({ burners: fc.constant(-1), ovens: fc.constant(1), cooks: fc.constant(1) }),
  fc.record({ burners: fc.constant(2), ovens: fc.constant(9), cooks: fc.constant(1) }),
  fc.record({ burners: fc.constant(9), ovens: fc.constant(1), cooks: fc.constant(2) }),
  fc.record({ burners: fc.constant(2), ovens: fc.constant(1), cooks: fc.constant(1.5) }),
  fc.record({ burners: fc.constant(2), ovens: fc.constant(1), cooks: fc.constant(0) }),
  fc.record({ burners: fc.constant('2'), ovens: fc.constant(1), cooks: fc.constant(1) }),
  fc.constantFrom('4 burners', '', 'kitchen'),
);

const arbBadServe: fc.Arbitrary<unknown> = fc.oneof(
  fc.constantFrom('24:00', '7:00', '19:60', '19:00\\n.PHONY: x', ''),
  fc.string().filter((value) => !/^(?:[01][0-9]|2[0-3]):[0-5][0-9]$/.test(value)),
);

/**
 * Like `arbDinnerfileOptions`, but each present value is, half the time, one that fails its
 * schema (typed by a cast here and nowhere else).
 */
export function arbAnyDinnerfileOptions(): fc.Arbitrary<DinnerfileOptions> {
  return fc
    .record(
      {
        kitchen: fc.oneof(arbKitchen() as fc.Arbitrary<unknown>, arbBadKitchen),
        serve: fc.oneof(arbServeTime() as fc.Arbitrary<unknown>, arbBadServe),
      },
      { requiredKeys: [] },
    )
    .map((options) => options as unknown as DinnerfileOptions);
}
'''
open(p,'w').write(s)

s+=r'''// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Dinnerfile spec (T1.3). Written by hand from `.kiro/specs/dinnerfile/design.md`, sections
// "Worst case" and "Sunday roast"; nothing here is produced by src/. A tab is written `\t`.

/**
 * The worst case of the dinnerfile design: free text holding every character that could turn a
 * title into Make or shell syntax, a dish called `dinner`, directive-word dish ids, a step id
 * that starts with `-`, a lone surrogate and a title that is just `-n`. It passes `PlanSchema`
 * and `checkPlan` gives it no issue. Steps and dishes are in non-canonical order on purpose.
 */
export const WORST_CASE: Plan = {
  title: 'Worst "case" \\ #',
  dishes: [
    { id: 'export', name: 'Export  ' },
    { id: 'dinner', name: 'Dinner itself' },
    { id: 'define', name: 'D\u00e9finir' },
  ],
  steps: [
    {
      id: 'define.y',
      dish: 'define',
      title: 'Line\r\nbreak\u0000 and \u2028end',
      minutes: 480,
      appliance: 'oven',
      handsOn: false,
      after: ['export.x'],
      holdMin: 480,
    },
    {
      id: 'export.x',
      dish: 'export',
      title:
        " It's $(MAKE) ${HOME} $$ `touch canary` $(shell touch canary) ; touch canary #1 \\ 50% a:=b\tc\nd\u202ee\ud800 \\",
      detail: 'Line one\nLine two "quoted" \\c',
      minutes: 5,
      appliance: 'burner',
      handsOn: false,
      after: ['--.--'],
      holdMin: 0,
    },
    {
      id: '--.--',
      dish: 'dinner',
      title: '-n',
      minutes: 1,
      appliance: 'none',
      handsOn: true,
      after: [],
      holdMin: null,
    },
  ],
};

const DINNERFILE_HEADER: readonly string[] = [
  '# Dinnerfile written by Make Dinner. GNU Make reads it:',
  '#   make -n -f Dinnerfile dinner    prints every step in an order that works',
  '#   make -n -f Dinnerfile <dish>    prints the steps one dish needs',
  '# The "# plan", "# dish", "# step", "# kitchen" and "# serve" lines hold the plan.',
];

/**
 * `toDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST)`, the complete text. Worked out by hand in the dinnerfile
 * design, "Sunday roast": canonical step order puts chicken.carve before chicken.prep and
 * chicken.rest before chicken.roast; terminal steps are chicken.carve, gravy.strain and
 * greens.toss, which are also the one last step of each dish.
 */
export const SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE: string =
  [
    ...DINNERFILE_HEADER,
    '',
    '# plan "Sunday roast"',
    '# dish chicken "Roast chicken"',
    '# dish gravy "Gravy"',
    '# dish greens "Spring greens"',
    '',
    '.PHONY: dinner chicken gravy greens chicken.carve chicken.prep chicken.rest chicken.roast gravy.roux gravy.simmer gravy.strain greens.boil greens.shred greens.toss',
    'dinner: chicken.carve gravy.strain greens.toss',
    '',
    'chicken: chicken.carve',
    'gravy: gravy.strain',
    'greens: greens.toss',
    '',
    '# step dish=chicken minutes=5 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=5 title="Carve the chicken"',
    'chicken.carve: chicken.rest',
    "\t@echo 'Carve the chicken' # chicken.carve",
    '',
    '# step dish=chicken minutes=10 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="Season and truss the chicken"',
    'chicken.prep:',
    "\t@echo 'Season and truss the chicken' # chicken.prep",
    '',
    '# step dish=chicken minutes=20 appliance=none handsOn=false hold=30 title="Rest the chicken under foil"',
    'chicken.rest: chicken.roast',
    "\t@echo 'Rest the chicken under foil' # chicken.rest",
    '',
    '# step dish=chicken minutes=75 appliance=oven handsOn=false hold=5 title="Roast the chicken"',
    'chicken.roast: chicken.prep',
    "\t@echo 'Roast the chicken' # chicken.roast",
    '',
    '# step dish=gravy minutes=2 appliance=burner handsOn=true hold=5 title="Make a roux in the roasting tin" detail="Use the juices left in the tin once the chicken is out."',
    'gravy.roux: chicken.roast',
    "\t@echo 'Make a roux in the roasting tin' # gravy.roux",
    '',
    '# step dish=gravy minutes=10 appliance=burner handsOn=false hold=5 title="Add stock and let the gravy thicken"',
    'gravy.simmer: gravy.roux',
    "\t@echo 'Add stock and let the gravy thicken' # gravy.simmer",
    '',
    '# step dish=gravy minutes=2 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=15 title="Strain the gravy into a jug"',
    'gravy.strain: gravy.simmer',
    "\t@echo 'Strain the gravy into a jug' # gravy.strain",
    '',
    '# step dish=greens minutes=4 appliance=burner handsOn=false hold=0 title="Cook the greens in salted water"',
    'greens.boil: greens.shred',
    "\t@echo 'Cook the greens in salted water' # greens.boil",
    '',
    '# step dish=greens minutes=5 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="Shred the greens"',
    'greens.shred:',
    "\t@echo 'Shred the greens' # greens.shred",
    '',
    '# step dish=greens minutes=1 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=5 title="Toss the greens with butter"',
    'greens.toss: greens.boil',
    "\t@echo 'Toss the greens with butter' # greens.toss",
  ].join('\n') + '\n';

/**
 * `make -n -f Dinnerfile dinner` on `SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE`. Worked out by hand from the
 * design: GNU Make visits prerequisites left to right, depth first, and (measured in the
 * design session) prints each step once.
 */
export const SUNDAY_ROAST_MAKE_DINNER: readonly string[] = [
  "echo 'Season and truss the chicken' # chicken.prep",
  "echo 'Roast the chicken' # chicken.roast",
  "echo 'Rest the chicken under foil' # chicken.rest",
  "echo 'Carve the chicken' # chicken.carve",
  "echo 'Make a roux in the roasting tin' # gravy.roux",
  "echo 'Add stock and let the gravy thicken' # gravy.simmer",
  "echo 'Strain the gravy into a jug' # gravy.strain",
  "echo 'Shred the greens' # greens.shred",
  "echo 'Cook the greens in salted water' # greens.boil",
  "echo 'Toss the greens with butter' # greens.toss",
];

/**
 * `make -n -f Dinnerfile gravy` on `SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE`: the gravy needs the roasting tin,
 * so the chicken's first two steps come first (dinnerfile design, "Sunday roast").
 */
export const SUNDAY_ROAST_MAKE_GRAVY: readonly string[] = [
  "echo 'Season and truss the chicken' # chicken.prep",
  "echo 'Roast the chicken' # chicken.roast",
  "echo 'Make a roux in the roasting tin' # gravy.roux",
  "echo 'Add stock and let the gravy thicken' # gravy.simmer",
  "echo 'Strain the gravy into a jug' # gravy.strain",
];

/**
 * `toDinnerfile(WORST_CASE)`, the complete text, from the design's "Worst case". Canonical step
 * order is `--.--`, `define.y`, `export.x` (`-` sorts before `d`); the dish `dinner` gets the
 * fixed comment line and no rule; the only terminal step is `define.y`. The echo argument of
 * `export.x` is its title's display form (tab and newline as spaces, U+202E and the lone
 * U+D800 as U+FFFD) with every `'` as `'\''` and every `$` as `$$` plus `''` before `(` or `{`.
 */
export const WORST_CASE_DINNERFILE: string =
  [
    ...DINNERFILE_HEADER,
    '',
    '# plan "Worst \\"case\\" \\\\ #"',
    '# dish define "D\u00e9finir"',
    '# dish dinner "Dinner itself"',
    '# dish export "Export  "',
    '',
    '.PHONY: dinner define export --.-- define.y export.x',
    'dinner: define.y',
    '',
    'define: define.y',
    '# No target for dish dinner: "dinner" makes the whole plan.',
    'export: export.x',
    '',
    '# step dish=dinner minutes=1 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="-n"',
    '--.--:',
    "\t@echo '-n' # --.--",
    '',
    '# step dish=define minutes=480 appliance=oven handsOn=false hold=480 title="Line\\r\\nbreak\\u0000 and \\u2028end"',
    'define.y: export.x',
    "\t@echo 'Line  break  and  end' # define.y",
    '',
    '# step dish=export minutes=5 appliance=burner handsOn=false hold=0 title=" It\'s $(MAKE) ${HOME} $$ `touch canary` $(shell touch canary) ; touch canary #1 \\\\ 50% a:=b\\tc\\nd\\u202ee\\ud800 \\\\" detail="Line one\\nLine two \\"quoted\\" \\\\c"',
    'export.x: --.--',
    "\t@echo ' It'\\''s $$''(MAKE) $$''{HOME} $$$$ `touch canary` $$''(shell touch canary) ; touch canary #1 \\ 50% a:=b c d\ufffde\ufffd \\' # export.x",
  ].join('\n') + '\n';

/**
 * `make -n -f Dinnerfile dinner` on `WORST_CASE_DINNERFILE` (measured in the design session,
 * GNU Make 3.81): Make expands each `$$` to `$` and prints the recipe line without the `@`.
 */
export const WORST_CASE_MAKE_DINNER: readonly string[] = [
  "echo '-n' # --.--",
  "echo ' It'\\''s $''(MAKE) $''{HOME} $$ `touch canary` $''(shell touch canary) ; touch canary #1 \\ 50% a:=b c d\ufffde\ufffd \\' # export.x",
  "echo 'Line  break  and  end' # define.y",
];
'''

    ): { minute: number; resource: ResourceName; count: number; limit: number }[] {
      const out: { minute: number; resource: ResourceName; count: number; limit: number }[] = [];
      if (plan.steps.length === 0) return out;
      let first = Infinity;
      let last = -Infinity;
      for (const s of plan.steps) {
        first = Math.min(first, startOf(starts, s));
        last = Math.max(last, endOf(starts, s));
      }
      for (let minute = first; minute < last; minute++) {
        for (const resource of RESOURCE_NAMES) {
          let count = 0;
          for (const s of plan.steps) {
            if (usesResource(s, resource) && activeAt(starts, s, minute)) count++;
          }
          const limit = limitOf(kitchen, resource);
          if (count > limit) out.push({ minute, resource, count, limit });
        }
      }
      return out;
    }

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Dinnerfile oracles (dinnerfile spec). Written from the design's tables, without looking at
// src/core/dinnerfile.ts. Stubs until the T1.4 tests have failed.

/** Every way of writing the same plan that the import accepts (requirements R6.6). */
export interface DinnerfileStyle {
  eol: 'lf' | 'crlf';
  bom: boolean;
  header: boolean;
  phony: 'one' | 'none' | 'per-rule';
  dinnerRule: boolean;
  dishRules: boolean;
  /** `echo` the exported line, `none` no recipe line, `true` `\t@true`, `fail` `\techo "$@" ; exit 1`. */
  recipes: 'echo' | 'none' | 'true' | 'fail';
  /** Appended to every line (white space at line ends). */
  lineEndSpace: string;
  /** A blank line after every nth line; 0 for none. */
  blankEvery: number;
  /** Unknown comments, cycled through and inserted after every `commentEvery` lines; 0 for none. */
  comments: readonly string[];
  commentEvery: number;
  /** Spaces before every comment and rule line. */
  indent: string;
  /** Write ` # note` after every rule line. */
  ruleNote: boolean;
  /** The order of the keys of a `# step` line, and of a `# kitchen` line. */
  stepKeys: readonly string[];
  kitchenKeys: readonly string[];
  /** Runs of SP and HTAB between fields and between prerequisites. */
  separator: string;
  spaceAfterColon: boolean;
  stepOrder: 'canonical' | 'reversed' | 'rotated';
  dishOrder: 'canonical' | 'reversed' | 'rotated';
  optionOrder: 'canonical' | 'reversed';
  /** The `# plan`, `# dish`, `# kitchen` and `# serve` lines after the step blocks. */
  metadataAtBottom: boolean;
  /** `\uXXXX` for every non-ASCII code point and `\/` for `/` in strings. */
  escapes: boolean;
  /** Repeat the first prerequisite of every rule that has one. */
  repeatPrerequisite: boolean;
}

  eol: 'lf',

  header: true,
  phony: 'one',

  dishRules: true,
  recipes: 'echo',
  lineEndSpace: '',
  blankEvery: 0,
  comments: [],
  commentEvery: 0,
  indent: '',
  ruleNote: false,
  stepKeys: ['dish', 'minutes', 'appliance', 'handsOn', 'hold', 'title', 'detail'],
  kitchenKeys: ['burners', 'ovens', 'cooks'],
  separator: ' ',
  spaceAfterColon: true,
  stepOrder: 'canonical',
  dishOrder: 'canonical',
  optionOrder: 'canonical',
  metadataAtBottom: false,
  escapes: false,
  repeatPrerequisite: false,
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Dinnerfile spec, T1.4: the dinnerfile oracles against the hand-written fixtures only.

const WORST_EXPORT_TITLE = WORST_CASE.steps.find((x) => x.id === 'export.x')?.title ?? '';
const WORST_DEFINE_TITLE = WORST_CASE.steps.find((x) => x.id === 'define.y')?.title ?? '';

describe('R10.2 oracleDinnerfile writes the hand-worked exports', () => {
  it('SUNDAY_ROAST with no options is SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE, character for character', () => {
    expect(oracleDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST, {})).toBe(SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE);
  });
  it('WORST_CASE with no options is WORST_CASE_DINNERFILE, character for character', () => {
    expect(oracleDinnerfile(WORST_CASE, {})).toBe(WORST_CASE_DINNERFILE);
  });
  it('passing CANONICAL_STYLE is the same as passing no style', () => {
    expect(oracleDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST, {}, CANONICAL_STYLE)).toBe(
      oracleDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST, {}),
    );
  });
  it('a kitchen and a serving time add exactly two lines after the plan line (lines 7 and 8)', () => {
    const lines = oracleDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST, {
      kitchen: HOME_KITCHEN,
      serve: '19:00',
    }).split('\n');
    const plain = SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE.split('\n');
    expect(lines[5]).toBe('# plan "Sunday roast"');
    expect(lines[6]).toBe('# kitchen burners=4 ovens=1 cooks=1');
    expect(lines[7]).toBe('# serve 19:00');
    expect([...lines.slice(0, 6), ...lines.slice(8)]).toEqual(plain);
  });
  it('a serving time alone is line 7', () => {
    const lines = oracleDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST, { serve: '09:05' }).split('\n');
    expect(lines[6]).toBe('# serve 09:05');
    expect(lines[7]).toBe('# dish chicken "Roast chicken"');
  });
});

describe('R10.2 oracleEncodeText and oracleEchoText on the worst-case titles', () => {
  it('the plan title encodes as "Worst \\"case\\" \\\\ #"', () => {
    expect(oracleEncodeText(WORST_CASE.title)).toBe('"Worst \\"case\\" \\\\ #"');
  });
  it('the define.y title: CR LF, NUL and U+2028 become escapes', () => {
    expect(oracleEncodeText(WORST_DEFINE_TITLE)).toBe('"Line\\r\\nbreak\\u0000 and \\u2028end"');
  });
  it('the export.x title: tab, newline, U+202E, a lone U+D800 and both backslashes', () => {
    expect(oracleEncodeText(WORST_EXPORT_TITLE)).toBe(
      '" It\'s $(MAKE) ${HOME} $$ `touch canary` $(shell touch canary) ; touch canary #1 \\\\ 50% a:=b\\tc\\nd\\u202ee\\ud800 \\\\"',
    );
  });
  it.each([
    ['\u0008\u000c', '"\\b\\f"'],
    ['\u0001\u001f', '"\\u0001\\u001f"'],
    ['\u007f\u0085\u009f', '"\\u007f\\u0085\\u009f"'],
    ['\u061c\u200e\u200f', '"\\u061c\\u200e\\u200f"'],
    ['\u202a\u202e\u2066\u2069', '"\\u202a\\u202e\\u2066\\u2069"'],
    ['\ufeff\u2028\u2029', '"\\ufeff\\u2028\\u2029"'],
    ['\ud800', '"\\ud800"'],
    ['\udfff', '"\\udfff"'],
    ['\u{1F525}', '"\u{1F525}"'],
    ['\ud83d\udd25', '"\u{1F525}"'],
    ['caf\u00e9 e\u0301', '"caf\u00e9 e\u0301"'],
    ["'$#`:=% ", '"\'$#`:=% "'],
    ['a/b', '"a/b"'],
  ])('%j encodes as %s', (text, expected) => {
    expect(oracleEncodeText(text)).toBe(expected);
  });
  it('the display form of the define.y title replaces CR, LF, NUL and U+2028 with spaces', () => {
    expect(oracleEchoText(WORST_DEFINE_TITLE)).toBe('Line  break  and  end');
  });
  it('the display form of the export.x title keeps its quotes and dollars, tab and newline become spaces, U+202E and U+D800 become U+FFFD', () => {
    expect(oracleEchoText(WORST_EXPORT_TITLE)).toBe(
      " It's $(MAKE) ${HOME} $$ `touch canary` $(shell touch canary) ; touch canary #1 \\ 50% a:=b c d\ufffde\ufffd \\",
    );
  });
  it.each([
    ['a\u001b[31mb', 'a [31mb'],
    ['a\u007fb\u0085c\u009fd', 'a b c d'],
    ['a\u2029b', 'a b'],
    ['\u061c\u200e\u200f\u202a\u202e\u2066\u2069\ufeff', '\ufffd'.repeat(8)],
    ['\ud800\udfff', '\u{10FFFF}'.length === 2 ? '\ud800\udfff' : ''],
    ['\u{1F525}', '\u{1F525}'],
  ])('the display form of %j is %j', (title, expected) => {
    // The pair \ud800\udfff is a valid pair (U+103FF), kept; every lone half becomes U+FFFD.
    expect(oracleEchoText(title)).toBe(expected);
  });
});

describe('R10.2 oracleMakeLines classifies the lines export may write, and nothing looser', () => {
  it('gives no "other" for any line of the two hand-written exports', () => {
    for (const text of [SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE, WORST_CASE_DINNERFILE]) {
      const lines = text.split('\n');
      const kinds = oracleMakeLines(text);
      expect(kinds.length).toBe(lines.length);
      expect(kinds.filter((k) => k === 'other')).toEqual([]);
    }
  });
  it('knows each kind on a small text', () => {
    const text = [
      '# note',
      '',
      '.PHONY: dinner a.b',
      'dinner: a.b',
      'a.b:',
      "\t@echo 'x' # a.b",
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    expect(oracleMakeLines(text)).toEqual([
      'comment',
      'blank',
      'rule',
      'rule',
      'rule',
      'recipe',
      'blank',
    ]);
  });
  it.each([
    ['export : x', 'white space before the colon'],
    ['X = 1', 'a variable'],
    ["\t@echo '$$(MAKE)' # a.b", 'a $$( not followed by an empty quote pair'],
    ["\t@echo '$(MAKE)' # a.b", 'a single $'],
    ['# a comment ending in a backslash \\', 'a comment ending in a backslash'],
    ['# trailing space ', 'white space at the end'],
    ['# bell \u0007', 'a control code point'],
    ['a.b: c.d ', 'white space at the end of a rule'],
    ['a.b:c.d', 'no space after the colon'],
    ['A.B: c.d', 'an upper-case target'],
    ['a.b: c.d  e.f', 'two spaces between names'],
    ['include other', 'a directive'],
    ["\t@echo 'x'", 'no step id'],
    ["\t@echo 'it's' # a.b", 'an unquoted apostrophe'],
    ['\t@true', 'a recipe that is not an echo'],
    [' # indented comment', 'a space before the comment'],
  ])('%j is other (%s)', (line) => {
    expect(oracleMakeLines(line)).toEqual(['other']);
  });
  it('"\\n" splits lines and a CR is part of its line, so a CRLF text is all "other"', () => {
    expect(oracleMakeLines('# a\r\n# b\r\n')).toEqual(['other', 'other', 'blank']);
  });
  it("accepts the quoting forms: '\\'' and $$'' before ( or {, and a lone $$", () => {
    expect(oracleMakeLines("\t@echo 'a'\\''b $$''(c) $$''{d} $$$$ e' # a.b")).toEqual(['recipe']);
  });
});

describe('R10.2 decodeEchoLine', () => {
  const recipeLines = WORST_CASE_DINNERFILE.split('\n').filter((l) => l.startsWith('\t@echo'));

  it('decodes the three worst-case recipe lines from the file', () => {
    expect(recipeLines.map((l) => decodeEchoLine(l, 'file'))).toEqual([
      { text: '-n', stepId: '--.--' },
      { text: 'Line  break  and  end', stepId: 'define.y' },
      { text: oracleEchoText(WORST_EXPORT_TITLE), stepId: 'export.x' },
    ]);
  });
  it('decodes the three worst-case printed lines, in the order Make printed them', () => {
    expect(WORST_CASE_MAKE_DINNER.map((l) => decodeEchoLine(l, 'printed'))).toEqual([
      { text: '-n', stepId: '--.--' },
      { text: oracleEchoText(WORST_EXPORT_TITLE), stepId: 'export.x' },
      { text: 'Line  break  and  end', stepId: 'define.y' },
    ]);
  });
  it('decodes the ten Sunday roast printed lines to their titles and step ids', () => {
    expect(SUNDAY_ROAST_MAKE_DINNER.map((l) => decodeEchoLine(l, 'printed')?.stepId)).toEqual([
      'chicken.prep',
      'chicken.roast',
      'chicken.rest',
      'chicken.carve',
      'gravy.roux',
      'gravy.simmer',
      'gravy.strain',
      'greens.shred',
      'greens.boil',
      'greens.toss',
    ]);
    expect(decodeEchoLine(SUNDAY_ROAST_MAKE_DINNER[3] ?? '', 'printed')?.text).toBe(
      'Carve the chicken',
    );
  });
  it('$$ in the file is $ after Make, and the "file" stage undoes it: $$$$ is two dollars', () => {
    expect(decodeEchoLine("\t@echo 'a $$$$ b' # x.y", 'file')).toEqual({
      text: 'a $$ b',
      stepId: 'x.y',
    });
    expect(decodeEchoLine("echo 'a $$ b' # x.y", 'printed')).toEqual({
      text: 'a $$ b',
      stepId: 'x.y',
    });
  });
  it("'\\'' is a quote and '' joins two quoted parts", () => {
    expect(decodeEchoLine("echo 'it'\\''s' # x.y", 'printed')?.text).toBe("it's");
    expect(decodeEchoLine("echo 'a''b' # x.y", 'printed')?.text).toBe('ab');
  });
  it.each([
    ['', 'empty'],
    ['echo hello # x.y', 'unquoted'],
    ["echo 'a' # X.Y", 'step id with capitals'],
    ["echo 'a' # x.y extra", 'text after the step id'],
    ["echo 'a'", 'no step id'],
    ["echo 'a # x.y", 'unterminated quote'],
    ["\techo 'a' # x.y", 'a tab in the printed stage'],
    ["\t@echo 'a'b' # x.y", 'a bare quote in the middle'],
  ])('returns null for %j (%s)', (line, _why) => {
    expect(decodeEchoLine(line, 'printed')).toBeNull();
  });
  it('returns null in the file stage when the line does not start with a tab and @', () => {
    expect(decodeEchoLine("echo 'a' # x.y", 'file')).toBeNull();
    expect(decodeEchoLine("\techo 'a' # x.y", 'file')).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('R10.2 oracleNeededSteps and oracleDishLastSteps', () => {
  it('the gravy needs the roasting tin: five steps, sorted', () => {
    expect(oracleNeededSteps(SUNDAY_ROAST, 'gravy')).toEqual([
      'chicken.prep',
      'chicken.roast',
      'gravy.roux',
      'gravy.simmer',
      'gravy.strain',
    ]);
    expect(oracleNeededSteps(SUNDAY_ROAST, 'gravy')).toEqual(
      SUNDAY_ROAST_MAKE_GRAVY.map((l) => decodeEchoLine(l, 'printed')?.stepId).sort(),
    );
  });
  it('the chicken needs only its own four steps; the greens only theirs', () => {
    expect(oracleNeededSteps(SUNDAY_ROAST, 'chicken')).toEqual([
      'chicken.carve',
      'chicken.prep',
      'chicken.rest',
      'chicken.roast',
    ]);
    expect(oracleNeededSteps(SUNDAY_ROAST, 'greens')).toEqual([
      'greens.boil',
      'greens.shred',
      'greens.toss',
    ]);
  });
  it('the worst case: the define dish needs all three steps (a chain)', () => {
    expect(oracleNeededSteps(WORST_CASE, 'define')).toEqual(['--.--', 'define.y', 'export.x']);
    expect(oracleNeededSteps(WORST_CASE, 'export')).toEqual(['--.--', 'export.x']);
  });
  it('last steps of the worst case: one per dish, including the dish called dinner', () => {
    expect(oracleDishLastSteps(WORST_CASE)).toEqual({
      define: ['define.y'],
      dinner: ['--.--'],
      export: ['export.x'],
    });
  });
  it('last steps of SUNDAY_ROAST: chicken.roast is not one, chicken.rest waits on it', () => {
    expect(oracleDishLastSteps(SUNDAY_ROAST)).toEqual({
      chicken: ['chicken.carve'],
      gravy: ['gravy.strain'],
      greens: ['greens.toss'],
    });
  });
});

i=s.index("export function oracleEncodeText(_text: string): string {")
s=s[:i]+r'''const STEP_ID_ORACLE = /^[a-z0-9-]+\.[a-z0-9-]+$/;

/** A lone surrogate is a one-unit string in a code point iteration. */
const isLoneSurrogate = (cp: number): boolean => cp >= 0xd800 && cp <= 0xdfff;
const isControl = (cp: number): boolean => cp < 0x20 || (cp >= 0x7f && cp <= 0x9f);
const isBidi = (cp: number): boolean =>
  cp === 0x61c ||
  cp === 0x200e ||
  cp === 0x200f ||
  (cp >= 0x202a && cp <= 0x202e) ||
  (cp >= 0x2066 && cp <= 0x2069);
const hex4 = (n: number): string => `\\u${n.toString(16).padStart(4, '0')}`;

/** The text of a metadata string, as the design's table writes it (with the quotes). */
function encode(text: string, escapes: boolean): string {
  let out = '"';
  for (const ch of text) {
    const cp = ch.codePointAt(0) ?? 0;
    if (ch === '"') out += '\\"';
    else if (ch === '\\') out += '\\\\';
    else if (cp === 8) out += '\\b';
    else if (cp === 12) out += '\\f';
    else if (cp === 10) out += '\\n';
    else if (cp === 13) out += '\\r';
    else if (cp === 9) out += '\\t';
    else if (
      isControl(cp) ||
      cp === 0x2028 ||
      cp === 0x2029 ||
      isBidi(cp) ||
      cp === 0xfeff ||
      isLoneSurrogate(cp)
    ) {
      out += hex4(cp);
    } else if (escapes && ch === '/') out += '\\/';
    else if (escapes && cp > 0x7f) {
      if (cp > 0xffff) {
        const offset = cp - 0x10000;
        out += hex4(0xd800 + Math.floor(offset / 0x400)) + hex4(0xdc00 + (offset % 0x400));
      } else out += hex4(cp);
    } else out += ch;
  }
  return out + '"';
}

export function oracleEncodeText(text: string): string {
  return encode(text, false);
}

/** R3.2: the title as one line of text, before shell and Make quoting. */
function displayForm(title: string): string {
  let out = '';
  for (const ch of title) {
    const cp = ch.codePointAt(0) ?? 0;
    if (isControl(cp) || cp === 0x2028 || cp === 0x2029) out += ' ';
    else if (isBidi(cp) || cp === 0xfeff || isLoneSurrogate(cp)) out += '\ufffd';
    else out += ch;
  }
  return out;
}

export function oracleEchoText(title: string): string {
  return displayForm(title);
}

/** R3.3: the single-quoted shell word's content, with Make's `$` doubled. */
function echoArgument(title: string): string {
  const points = [...displayForm(title)];
  let out = '';
  points.forEach((ch, i) => {
    if (ch === "'") out += "'\\''";
    else if (ch === '$') {
      out += '$$';
      const next = points[i + 1];
      if (next === '(' || next === '{') out += "''";
    } else out += ch;
  });
  return out;
}

const lessThan = (a: string, b: string): number => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0);

/** Distinct values in first-seen order. */
const distinct = (values: readonly string[]): string[] => {
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const v of values) if (!out.includes(v)) out.push(v);
  return out;
};

/** The oracle's own canonical plan: dishes and steps by id, each `after` sorted and distinct. */
function canonical(plan: Plan): Plan {
  return {
    title: plan.title,
    dishes: [...plan.dishes].sort((a, b) => lessThan(a.id, b.id)),
    steps: [...plan.steps]
      .map((step) => ({ ...step, after: distinct([...step.after].sort(lessThan)) }))
      .sort((a, b) => lessThan(a.id, b.id)),
  };
}

function terminalIds(plan: Plan): string[] {
  const used: string[] = [];
  for (const step of plan.steps) used.push(...step.after);
  return plan.steps.filter((s) => !used.includes(s.id)).map((s) => s.id);
}

function lastStepIds(plan: Plan, dishId: string): string[] {
  const mine = plan.steps.filter((s) => s.dish === dishId);
  return mine
    .filter((s) => !mine.some((u) => u.after.includes(s.id)))
    .map((s) => s.id)
    .sort(lessThan);
}

/** R1.7: for every declared dish, the steps of the dish that no other step of it waits on. */
export function oracleDishLastSteps(plan: Plan): Record<string, string[]> {
  const out: Record<string, string[]> = {};
  for (const dish of canonical(plan).dishes) out[dish.id] = lastStepIds(canonical(plan), dish.id);
  return out;
}

/**
 * R9.2: the steps of the dish and, again and again, the prerequisites of steps already in the
 * set, until nothing is added. Sorted.
 */
export function oracleNeededSteps(plan: Plan, dishId: string): string[] {
  const needed = plan.steps.filter((s) => s.dish === dishId).map((s) => s.id);
  for (let changed = true; changed; ) {
    changed = false;
    for (const step of plan.steps) {
      if (!needed.includes(step.id)) continue;
      for (const p of step.after) {
        if (!needed.includes(p) && plan.steps.some((s) => s.id === p)) {
          needed.push(p);
          changed = true;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return needed.sort(lessThan);
}

const HEADER_LINES: readonly string[] = [
  '# Dinnerfile written by Make Dinner. GNU Make reads it:',
  '#   make -n -f Dinnerfile dinner    prints every step in an order that works',
  '#   make -n -f Dinnerfile <dish>    prints the steps one dish needs',
  '# The "# plan", "# dish", "# step", "# kitchen" and "# serve" lines hold the plan.',
];

type LineType = 'comment' | 'rule' | 'recipe' | 'blank';
interface OutLine {
  type: LineType;
  text: string;
}

const reorder = <T>(items: readonly T[], how: 'canonical' | 'reversed' | 'rotated'): T[] => {
  if (how === 'reversed') return [...items].reverse();
  if (how === 'rotated') return [...items.slice(1), ...items.slice(0, 1)];
  return [...items];
};

/**
 * R1: the text of `toDinnerfile(plan, options)` when the style is `CANONICAL_STYLE`; in
 * another style, the same plan written in one of the ways R6.6 says the import accepts.
 */
export function oracleDinnerfile(
  plan: Plan,
  options: DinnerfileOptions,
  style: DinnerfileStyle = CANONICAL_STYLE,
): string {
  const p = canonical(plan);
  const sep = style.separator;
  const str = (text: string): string => encode(text, style.escapes);
  const lines: OutLine[] = [];
  const add = (type: LineType, text: string): void => {
    lines.push({ type, text });
  };
  const rule = (target: string, prereqs: readonly string[]): string => {
    const names = style.repeatPrerequisite && prereqs.length > 0 ? [prereqs[0] ?? '', ...prereqs] : prereqs;
    return `${target}:${names.length > 0 ? (style.spaceAfterColon ? sep : '') + names.join(sep) : ''}`;
  };

  const metadata = (): void => {
    add('comment', `#${sep}plan${sep}${str(p.title)}`);
    const optionLines: [string, string][] = [];
    const k = options.kitchen;
    if (k !== undefined) {
      const values: Record<string, number> = { burners: k.burners, ovens: k.ovens, cooks: k.cooks };
      optionLines.push([
        'kitchen',
        `#${sep}kitchen${style.kitchenKeys.map((key) => `${sep}${key}=${values[key] ?? 0}`).join('')}`,
      ]);
    }
    if (options.serve !== undefined) {
      optionLines.push(['serve', `#${sep}serve${sep}${options.serve}`]);
    }
    for (const [, text] of reorder(optionLines, style.optionOrder)) add('comment', text);
    for (const dish of reorder(p.dishes, style.dishOrder)) {
      add('comment', `#${sep}dish${sep}${dish.id}${sep}${str(dish.name)}`);
    }
  };

  if (style.header) {
    for (const text of HEADER_LINES) add('comment', text);
    add('blank', '');
  }
  if (!style.metadataAtBottom) {
    metadata();
    add('blank', '');
  }
  const dishIds = distinct(p.dishes.map((d) => d.id));
  const phonyNames = [
    'dinner',
    ...dishIds.filter((id) => id !== 'dinner'),
    ...p.steps.map((s) => s.id),
  ];
  if (style.phony === 'one') add('rule', `.PHONY:${sep}${phonyNames.join(sep)}`);
  const phonyFor = (target: string): void => {
    if (style.phony === 'per-rule') add('rule', `.PHONY:${sep}${target}`);
  };
  if (style.dinnerRule) {
    phonyFor('dinner');
    add('rule', rule('dinner', terminalIds(p)));
  }
  add('blank', '');
  if (style.dishRules) {
    for (const id of dishIds) {
      if (id === 'dinner') {
        add('comment', '# No target for dish dinner: "dinner" makes the whole plan.');
      } else {
        phonyFor(id);
        add('rule', rule(id, lastStepIds(p, id)));
      }
    }
  }
  for (const step of reorder(p.steps, style.stepOrder)) {
    add('blank', '');
    phonyFor(step.id);
    const values: Record<string, string | undefined> = {
      dish: step.dish,
      minutes: String(step.minutes),
      appliance: step.appliance,
      handsOn: String(step.handsOn),
      hold: step.holdMin === null ? 'keeps' : String(step.holdMin),
      title: str(step.title),
      detail: step.detail === undefined ? undefined : str(step.detail),
    };
    const fields = style.stepKeys
      .filter((key) => values[key] !== undefined)
      .map((key) => `${sep}${key}=${values[key] ?? ''}`)
      .join('');
    add('comment', `#${sep}step${fields}`);
    add('rule', rule(step.id, step.after));
    if (style.recipes === 'echo') {
      add('recipe', `\t@echo '${echoArgument(step.title)}' # ${step.id}`);
    } else if (style.recipes === 'true') add('recipe', '\t@true');
    else if (style.recipes === 'fail') add('recipe', '\techo "$@" ; exit 1');
  }
  if (style.metadataAtBottom) {
    add('blank', '');
    metadata();
  }

  const out: string[] = [];
  let commentIndex = 0;
  lines.forEach((line, i) => {
    let text = line.text;
    if (line.type === 'comment' || line.type === 'rule') text = style.indent + text;
    if (line.type === 'rule' && style.ruleNote) text += ' # note';
    out.push(text + style.lineEndSpace);
    if (style.blankEvery > 0 && (i + 1) % style.blankEvery === 0) out.push(style.lineEndSpace);
    if (style.commentEvery > 0 && (i + 1) % style.commentEvery === 0 && style.comments.length > 0) {
      const note = style.comments[commentIndex % style.comments.length] ?? '';
      commentIndex++;
      out.push(style.indent + note + style.lineEndSpace);
    }
  });
  const eol = style.eol === 'crlf' ? '\r\n' : '\n';
  return (style.bom ? '\ufeff' : '') + out.join(eol) + eol;
}

export type MakeLineKind = 'blank' | 'comment' | 'rule' | 'recipe' | 'other';

const RULE_LINE = /^(?:\.PHONY|[a-z0-9.-]+):(?: [a-z0-9.-]+)*$/;
const RECIPE_PREFIX = "\t@echo '";

function hasControl(text: string): boolean {
  for (const ch of text) if (isControl(ch.codePointAt(0) ?? 0)) return true;
  return false;
}

/** Whether `line` is a recipe line in the form export writes: the argument's tokens only. */
function isEchoRecipe(line: string): boolean {
  if (!line.startsWith(RECIPE_PREFIX)) return false;
  let i = RECIPE_PREFIX.length;
  while (i < line.length) {
    const ch = line.charAt(i);
    if (ch === "'") {
      if (line.startsWith("'\\''", i)) {
        i += 4;
        continue;
      }
      const rest = line.slice(i + 1);
      return rest.startsWith(' # ') && STEP_ID_ORACLE.test(rest.slice(3));
    }
    if (ch === '$') {
      if (line.startsWith("$$''(", i) || line.startsWith("$$''{", i)) {
        i += 4;
        continue;
      }
      const after = line.charAt(i + 2);
      if (line.startsWith('$$', i) && after !== '(' && after !== '{') {
        i += 2;
        continue;
      }
      return false;
    }
    if (isControl(ch.charCodeAt(0))) return false;
    i += 1;
  }
  return false;
}

/** The kind of each LF-separated line, by the forms export may write and nothing looser. */
export function oracleMakeLines(text: string): MakeLineKind[] {
  return text.split('\n').map((line): MakeLineKind => {
    if (line === '') return 'blank';
    if (/[ \t]$/.test(line)) return 'other';
    if (line.startsWith('#')) {
      return hasControl(line) || line.endsWith('\\') ? 'other' : 'comment';
    }
    if (RULE_LINE.test(line)) return 'rule';
    if (isEchoRecipe(line)) return 'recipe';
    return 'other';
  });
}

/**
 * The text and step id of an echo line, or null when it is not one. The `file` stage takes a
 * recipe line as written (HTAB, `@`, and Make's `$$` meaning `$`); `printed` takes what
 * `make -n` prints (`echo '...' # id`).
 */
export function decodeEchoLine(
  line: string,
  stage: 'file' | 'printed',
): { text: string; stepId: string } | null {
  let rest = line;
  if (stage === 'file') {
    if (!rest.startsWith('\t@')) return null;
    rest = rest.slice(2).split('$$').join('$');
  }
  if (!rest.startsWith("echo '")) return null;
  let i = "echo '".length;
  let text = '';
  while (i < rest.length) {
    const ch = rest.charAt(i);
    if (ch !== "'") {
      text += ch;
      i += 1;
      continue;
    }
    if (rest.startsWith("'\\''", i)) {
      text += "'";
      i += 4;
    } else if (rest.charAt(i + 1) === "'") {
      i += 2;
    } else {
      const tail = rest.slice(i + 1);
      if (!tail.startsWith(' # ')) return null;
      const stepId = tail.slice(3);
      return STEP_ID_ORACLE.test(stepId) ? { text, stepId } : null;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function arbDinnerfileStyle(_plan: Plan): fc.Arbitrary<DinnerfileStyle> {

export function arbDinnerfileLikeText(): fc.Arbitrary<string> {

export interface BreakingCase {
  /** The canonical export with exactly one meaning-changing edit. */
  text: string;
  /** The one error import must report, and where. */
  code: DinnerfileErrorCode;
  line: number | null;
  /** The row of the design's edit table, for the coverage check. */
  edit: string;
}

export function arbBreakingEdit(_plan: Plan): fc.Arbitrary<BreakingCase> {
  return fc.constant({ text: '', code: 'missing_plan', line: null, edit: 'stub' });
}

describe('R10.1 (dinnerfile) arbBreakingEdit', () => {
  const plans = [SUNDAY_ROAST, WORST_CASE, ...sampleOf(arbPlan(), 40)];
  const cases = plans.flatMap((plan) => sampleOf(arbBreakingEdit(plan), 60));

  it('every case has a text and a line that is null (missing_plan only) or inside the text', () => {
    expect(cases.length).toBe(plans.length * 60);
    for (const c of cases) {
      const lines = c.text.split('\n');
      if (c.line === null) {
        expect(c.code).toBe('missing_plan');
      } else {
        expect(c.line).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
        expect(c.line).toBeLessThanOrEqual(lines.length);
      }
    }
  });
  it('the line points at the edited line: the form each edit leaves there', () => {
    for (const c of cases) {
      if (c.line === null) continue;
      const line = c.text.split('\n')[c.line - 1] ?? '';
      switch (c.code) {
        case 'target_without_step':
        case 'duplicate_target':
          expect(oracleMakeLines(line)).toEqual(['rule']);
          expect(line.startsWith('.PHONY')).toBe(false);
          break;
        case 'step_without_target':
        case 'missing_key':
        case 'bad_value':
          expect(line.startsWith('# step ')).toBe(true);
          break;
        case 'unknown_key':
          expect(line.startsWith('# step ') && line.includes(' extra=1')).toBe(true);
          break;
        case 'duplicate_metadata':
          expect(line.startsWith('# plan ')).toBe(true);
          break;
        case 'dish_target_mismatch':
          expect(oracleMakeLines(line)).toEqual(['rule']);
          expect(line.includes('.')).toBe(false);
          break;
        case 'dinner_mismatch':
          expect(line.startsWith('dinner:')).toBe(true);
          break;
        case 'line_continuation':
          expect(line.endsWith(' \\')).toBe(true);
          break;
        case 'unsupported_line':
          expect(['X = 1', 'include other', 'a.x : b.y', 'a.x b.y:']).toContain(line);
          break;
        case 'recipe_before_rule':
          expect(line).toBe('\t@true');
          expect(c.text.split('\n')[c.line] ?? '').toMatch(/^\.PHONY:/);
          break;
        default:
          throw new Error(`unexpected code ${c.code}`);
      }
    }
  });
  it('the edit leaves the text different from the canonical export of its plan', () => {
    for (const plan of plans.slice(0, 10)) {
      const canonicalText = oracleDinnerfile(plan, {});
      for (const c of sampleOf(arbBreakingEdit(plan), 40)) expect(c.text).not.toBe(canonicalText);
    }
  });
  it('every row of the design table occurs, with its code', () => {
    const byEdit = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
    for (const c of cases) {
      const codes = byEdit.get(c.edit) ?? new Set<string>();
      codes.add(c.code);
      byEdit.set(c.edit, codes);
    }
    const expected: Record<string, string> = {
      'remove-step-line': 'target_without_step',
      'remove-rule-line': 'step_without_target',
      'duplicate-rule': 'duplicate_target',
      'unknown-key': 'unknown_key',
      'bad-minutes': 'bad_value',
      'bad-handsOn': 'bad_value',
      'bad-hold': 'bad_value',
      'remove-minutes': 'missing_key',
      'repeat-plan': 'duplicate_metadata',
      'remove-plan': 'missing_plan',
      'dish-rule-extra': 'dish_target_mismatch',
      'dinner-rule-extra': 'dinner_mismatch',
      'line-continuation': 'line_continuation',
      'unsupported-line': 'unsupported_line',
      'recipe-before-rule': 'recipe_before_rule',
    };
    expect([...byEdit.keys()].sort()).toEqual(Object.keys(expected).sort());
    for (const [edit, code] of Object.entries(expected)) {
      expect([edit, [...(byEdit.get(edit) ?? [])]]).toEqual([edit, [code]]);
    }
  });
  it('the bad minutes of the table all occur, and the four unsupported lines', () => {
    const texts = cases.map((c) => c.text);
    for (const bad of ['1.5', '-3', '007', '', 'abc', '1e3', '1234567']) {
      expect(texts.some((t) => t.includes(` minutes=${bad} `))).toBe(true);
    }
    for (const line of ['X = 1', 'include other', 'a.x : b.y', 'a.x b.y:']) {
      expect(texts.some((t) => t.split('\n').includes(line))).toBe(true);
    }
    expect(texts.some((t) => t.includes(' handsOn=yes '))).toBe(true);
    expect(texts.some((t) => t.includes(' hold=keep '))).toBe(true);
  });
  it('edits that need a shape the plan lacks are not drawn for it', () => {
    // SUNDAY_ROAST has a non-terminal step and dishes with several steps; a one-step plan has
    // neither a non-last step nor a non-terminal one.

      steps: [
        {
          id: 'a.x',
          dish: 'a',
          title: 'Do it',
          minutes: 5,
          appliance: 'none',
          handsOn: true,
          after: [],
          holdMin: null,
        },
      ],
    };
    const edits = new Set(sampleOf(arbBreakingEdit(single), 200).map((c) => c.edit));
    expect(edits.has('dish-rule-extra')).toBe(false);
    expect(edits.has('dinner-rule-extra')).toBe(false);
    expect(edits.has('duplicate-rule')).toBe(false);
    expect(edits.has('bad-minutes')).toBe(true);
  });
});

p='test/support/arbitraries.ts'
s=open(p).read()
i=s.index("// Stubs until the T1.5 meta tests have failed.")
s=s[:i]+r'''const COMMENT_POOL: readonly string[] = [
  '#',
  '# note',
  '#   Plan "x"',
  '# steps below',
  '# plan2',
  '#stepping',
];
const STEP_KEYS: readonly string[] = [
  'dish',
  'minutes',
  'appliance',
  'handsOn',
  'hold',
  'title',
  'detail',
];
const KITCHEN_KEYS: readonly string[] = ['burners', 'ovens', 'cooks'];

/**
 * A way of writing the same plan that the import must read the same (R6.6): line endings, BOM,
 * header, `.PHONY` forms, missing dish and dinner rules, other recipe lines, white space,
 * blank lines, unknown comments, key and block orders, escapes, a repeated prerequisite. The
 * rates of blank lines and comments grow with the size of the plan, up to every sixth line.
 */
export function arbDinnerfileStyle(plan: Plan): fc.Arbitrary<DinnerfileStyle> {
  const maxEvery = Math.min(6, 2 + plan.steps.length);
  const order = fc.constantFrom<DinnerfileStyle['stepOrder'][]>('canonical', 'reversed', 'rotated');
  return fc.record({
    eol: fc.constantFrom<DinnerfileStyle['eol'][]>('lf', 'crlf'),
    bom: fc.boolean(),
    header: fc.boolean(),
    phony: fc.constantFrom<DinnerfileStyle['phony'][]>('one', 'none', 'per-rule'),
    dinnerRule: fc.boolean(),
    dishRules: fc.boolean(),
    recipes: fc.constantFrom<DinnerfileStyle['recipes'][]>('echo', 'none', 'true', 'fail'),
    lineEndSpace: fc.constantFrom('', ' ', '\t', '  \t'),
    blankEvery: fc.integer({ min: 0, max: maxEvery }),
    comments: fc.shuffledSubarray([...COMMENT_POOL]),
    commentEvery: fc.integer({ min: 0, max: maxEvery }),
    indent: fc.constantFrom('', ' ', '   '),
    ruleNote: fc.boolean(),
    stepKeys: arbPermutation(STEP_KEYS),
    kitchenKeys: arbPermutation(KITCHEN_KEYS),
    separator: fc.constantFrom(' ', '  ', '\t', ' \t ', '\t\t'),
    spaceAfterColon: fc.boolean(),
    stepOrder: order,
    dishOrder: order,
    optionOrder: fc.constantFrom<DinnerfileStyle['optionOrder'][]>('canonical', 'reversed'),
    metadataAtBottom: fc.boolean(),
    escapes: fc.boolean(),
    repeatPrerequisite: fc.boolean(),
  });
}

/** Valid and broken metadata, rule and recipe lines, to be joined into Dinnerfile-like text. */
const LIKE_LINES: readonly string[] = [
  '# plan "Sunday roast"',
  '# plan Sunday',
  '# plan "unterminated',
  '# dish a "A"',
  '# dish A "A"',
  '# dish a',
  '# dish a "A" extra',
  '# step dish=a minutes=5 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="Do it"',
  '# step dish=a minutes=1.5 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="Do it"',
  '# step dish=a minutes=abc appliance=oven handsOn=yes hold=keep title="Do it"',
  '# step dish=a minutes=5 minutes=6 title="Do it" extra=1',
  '# step dish=a title="unterminated',
  '# step dish=a title="bad \\q escape"',
  '# step title="\\ud800" detail="\\u00e9"',
  '#step dish=a',
  '#stepping',
  '# Step dish=a',
  '# kitchen burners=4 ovens=1 cooks=1',
  '# kitchen burners=-1 ovens=1',
  '# serve 19:00',
  '# serve 25:00',
  '# serve',
  '# a comment',
  ' # indented comment',
  '#',
  'a.x:',
  'a.x: b.y',
  'a.x: b.y c.z # note',
  'a.x:b.y',
  'a.x : b.y',
  'a.x b.y:',
  'a.x:: b.y',
  'a: a.x',
  'dinner: a.x',
  '.PHONY: dinner a a.x',
  '.PHONY:',
  'A.X: B.Y',
  '-.-:',
  'export: export.x',
  '$(X): y',
  '%.o: %.c',
  'a.x: $(Y)',
  'a.x: b.y \\',
  'X = 1',
  'include other',
  'ifeq (a,b)',
  "\t@echo 'hi' # a.x",
  '\t# step dish=x minutes=1',
  '\t@touch canary',
  '   ',
  '',
  ':',
  ': x',
];

const arbLikeLines: fc.Arbitrary<string> = fc
  .tuple(
    fc.array(fc.constantFrom(...LIKE_LINES), { minLength: 0, maxLength: 40 }),
    fc.constantFrom('\n', '\r\n', '\r'),
  )
  .map(([lines, eol]) => lines.join(eol));

interface Mutation {
  kind: 'insert' | 'delete' | 'swap';
  where: 'start' | 'end' | 'anywhere';
  a: number;
  b: number;
  ch: string;
}

const arbMutation: fc.Arbitrary<Mutation> = fc.record({
  kind: fc.constantFrom<Mutation['kind'][]>('insert', 'delete', 'swap'),
  where: fc.constantFrom<Mutation['where'][]>('start', 'end', 'anywhere', 'anywhere'),
  a: fc.nat(),
  b: fc.nat(),
  ch: fc.string({ unit: 'binary', minLength: 1, maxLength: 1 }),
});

function mutate(text: string, m: Mutation): string {
  if (m.kind === 'swap') {
    const lines = text.split('\n');
    if (lines.length < 2) return text;
    const i = m.a % lines.length;
    const j = m.b % lines.length;
    const copy = [...lines];
    copy[i] = lines[j] ?? '';
    copy[j] = lines[i] ?? '';
    return copy.join('\n');
  }
  const at = m.where === 'start' ? 0 : m.where === 'end' ? text.length : m.a % (text.length + 1);
  if (m.kind === 'insert') return text.slice(0, at) + m.ch + text.slice(at);
  const from = m.where === 'end' ? Math.max(0, text.length - 1) : Math.min(at, text.length);
  return text.slice(0, from) + text.slice(from + 1);
}

const arbMutatedExport: fc.Arbitrary<string> = arbPlan()
  .chain((plan) =>
    fc.tuple(
      fc.constant(plan),
      arbDinnerfileOptions(),
      fc.array(arbMutation, { minLength: 1, maxLength: 3 }),
    ),
  )
  .map(([plan, options, mutations]) =>
    mutations.reduce(mutate, oracleDinnerfile(plan, options)),
  );

/**
 * Text for the totality property of import: arbitrary binary strings, lines from a vocabulary
 * of valid and broken Dinnerfile lines joined by LF, CRLF or CR, and an export with one to
 * three random insertions, deletions or line swaps.
 */
export function arbDinnerfileLikeText(): fc.Arbitrary<string> {
  return fc.oneof(
    { weight: 2, arbitrary: fc.string({ unit: 'binary' }) },
    { weight: 3, arbitrary: arbLikeLines },
    { weight: 3, arbitrary: arbMutatedExport },
  );
}

export interface BreakingCase {
  /** The canonical export with exactly one meaning-changing edit. */
  text: string;
  /** The one error import must report, and where (computed on the edited text). */
  code: DinnerfileErrorCode;
  line: number | null;
  /** The row of the design's edit table, for the coverage check. */
  edit: string;
}

interface EditResult {
  lines: string[];
  /** 0-based index in `lines` of the line the error is on, or null. */
  at: number | null;
  code: DinnerfileErrorCode;
  edit: string;
}

const BAD_MINUTES: readonly string[] = ['1.5', '-3', '007', '', 'abc', '1e3', '1234567'];
const UNSUPPORTED_LINES: readonly string[] = ['X = 1', 'include other', 'a.x : b.y', 'a.x b.y:'];

/**
 * The canonical export of `plan` with exactly one meaning-changing edit from the design's
 * table, and the one error import must report. Edits that need a shape the plan lacks (a dish
 * with a non-last step, a non-terminal step, a step with a free id to add) are not drawn.
 * Note: `duplicate-rule` copies the step's `# step` line along with the rule (a rule alone
 * would also be a `target_without_step`, and the property says exactly one error).
 */
export function arbBreakingEdit(plan: Plan): fc.Arbitrary<BreakingCase> {
  const base = oracleDinnerfile(plan, {}).split('\n');
  const ids = plan.steps.map((st) => st.id);
  const ruleIdxOf = (id: string): number => base.findIndex((l) => l.startsWith(`${id}:`));
  const afterOf = (id: string): readonly string[] =>
    plan.steps.find((st) => st.id === id)?.after ?? [];
  const freeIds = (id: string): string[] =>
    ids.filter((other) => other !== id && !afterOf(id).includes(other));
  const lastSteps = oracleDishLastSteps(plan);
  const nonLastOf = (dishId: string): string[] =>
    plan.steps
      .filter((st) => st.dish === dishId && !(lastSteps[dishId] ?? []).includes(st.id))
      .map((st) => st.id);
  const dishesWithNonLast = Object.keys(lastSteps).filter(
    (d) => d !== 'dinner' && nonLastOf(d).length > 0,
  );
  const used = new Set(plan.steps.flatMap((st) => st.after));
  const nonTerminal = ids.filter((id) => used.has(id));
  const pick = <T>(items: readonly T[], n: number): T => items[n % items.length] as T;
  // `as T` above is sound: every call site passes a non-empty list (the edit is drawn only then).

  const edits: [string, (n: number, m: number) => EditResult][] = [
    [
      'remove-step-line',
      (n) => {
        const r = ruleIdxOf(pick(ids, n));
        const lines = base.filter((_, i) => i !== r - 1);
        return { lines, at: r - 1, code: 'target_without_step', edit: 'remove-step-line' };
      },
    ],
    [
      'remove-rule-line',
      (n) => {
        const r = ruleIdxOf(pick(ids, n));
        const lines = base.filter((_, i) => i !== r);
        return { lines, at: r - 1, code: 'step_without_target', edit: 'remove-rule-line' };
      },
    ],
    [
      'unknown-key',
      (n) => {
        const r = ruleIdxOf(pick(ids, n)) - 1;
        const lines = base.map((l, i) => (i === r ? `${l} extra=1` : l));
        return { lines, at: r, code: 'unknown_key', edit: 'unknown-key' };
      },
    ],
    [
      'bad-minutes',
      (n, m) => {
        const r = ruleIdxOf(pick(ids, n)) - 1;
        const bad = pick(BAD_MINUTES, m);
        const lines = base.map((l, i) => (i === r ? l.replace(/ minutes=\d+/, ` minutes=${bad}`) : l));
        return { lines, at: r, code: 'bad_value', edit: 'bad-minutes' };
      },
    ],
    [
      'bad-handsOn',
      (n) => {
        const r = ruleIdxOf(pick(ids, n)) - 1;
        const lines = base.map((l, i) =>
          i === r ? l.replace(/ handsOn=(?:true|false)/, ' handsOn=yes') : l,
        );
        return { lines, at: r, code: 'bad_value', edit: 'bad-handsOn' };
      },
    ],
    [
      'bad-hold',
      (n) => {
        const r = ruleIdxOf(pick(ids, n)) - 1;
        const lines = base.map((l, i) =>
          i === r ? l.replace(/ hold=(?:\d+|keeps)/, ' hold=keep') : l,
        );
        return { lines, at: r, code: 'bad_value', edit: 'bad-hold' };
      },
    ],
    [
      'remove-minutes',
      (n) => {
        const r = ruleIdxOf(pick(ids, n)) - 1;
        const lines = base.map((l, i) => (i === r ? l.replace(/ minutes=\d+/, '') : l));
        return { lines, at: r, code: 'missing_key', edit: 'remove-minutes' };
      },
    ],
    [
      'repeat-plan',
      () => {
        const planIdx = base.findIndex((l) => l.startsWith('# plan '));
        let lastDish = -1;
        base.forEach((l, i) => {
          if (l.startsWith('# dish ')) lastDish = i;
        });
        const lines = [...base];
        lines.splice(lastDish + 1, 0, base[planIdx] ?? '');
        return { lines, at: lastDish + 1, code: 'duplicate_metadata', edit: 'repeat-plan' };
      },
    ],
    [
      'remove-plan',
      () => {
        const lines = base.filter((l) => !l.startsWith('# plan '));
        return { lines, at: null, code: 'missing_plan', edit: 'remove-plan' };
      },
    ],
    [
      'line-continuation',
      (n) => {
        const r = n % (base.length - 1); // not the empty piece after the final LF
        const lines = base.map((l, i) => (i === r ? `${l} \\` : l));
        return { lines, at: r, code: 'line_continuation', edit: 'line-continuation' };
      },
    ],
    [
      'unsupported-line',
      (n, m) => {
        const r = ruleIdxOf(pick(ids, n)) - 1;
        const lines = [...base];
        lines.splice(r, 0, pick(UNSUPPORTED_LINES, m));
        return { lines, at: r, code: 'unsupported_line', edit: 'unsupported-line' };
      },
    ],
    [
      'recipe-before-rule',
      () => {
        const phony = base.findIndex((l) => l.startsWith('.PHONY:'));
        const lines = [...base];
        lines.splice(phony, 0, '\t@true');
        return { lines, at: phony, code: 'recipe_before_rule', edit: 'recipe-before-rule' };
      },
    ],
  ];
  const withExtra = ids.filter((id) => freeIds(id).length > 0);
  if (withExtra.length > 0) {
    edits.push([
      'duplicate-rule',
      (n, m) => {
        const id = pick(withExtra, n);
        const r = ruleIdxOf(id);
        const copy = [
          '',
          base[r - 1] ?? '',
          `${base[r] ?? ''} ${pick(freeIds(id), m)}`,
          base[r + 1] ?? '',
        ];
        const lines = [...base.slice(0, -1), ...copy, ''];
        return {
          lines,
          at: lines.length - 3,
          code: 'duplicate_target',
          edit: 'duplicate-rule',
        };
      },
    ]);
  }
  if (dishesWithNonLast.length > 0) {
    edits.push([
      'dish-rule-extra',
      (n, m) => {
        const dishId = pick(dishesWithNonLast, n);
        const r = base.findIndex((l) => l.startsWith(`${dishId}:`));
        const lines = base.map((l, i) => (i === r ? `${l} ${pick(nonLastOf(dishId), m)}` : l));
        return { lines, at: r, code: 'dish_target_mismatch', edit: 'dish-rule-extra' };
      },
    ]);
  }
  if (nonTerminal.length > 0) {
    edits.push([
      'dinner-rule-extra',
      (n) => {
        const r = base.findIndex((l) => l.startsWith('dinner:'));
        const lines = base.map((l, i) => (i === r ? `${l} ${pick(nonTerminal, n)}` : l));
        return { lines, at: r, code: 'dinner_mismatch', edit: 'dinner-rule-extra' };
      },
    ]);
  }
  return fc
    .tuple(fc.constantFrom(...edits), fc.nat(), fc.nat())
    .map(([[, make], n, m]): BreakingCase => {
      const { lines, at, code, edit } = make(n, m);
      return { text: lines.join('\n'), code, line: at === null ? null : at + 1, edit };
    });
}
'''

open(p,'w').write(s)

// A GNU Make runner for the Dinnerfile tests (dinnerfile design, "Make runner"). Stubs until the
// T1.6 meta tests have failed.

export interface MakeRun {
  status: number | null;
  stdout: string;
  stderr: string;
}

export function gnuMakeVersion(_path?: string): string {
  return '';
}

export function makeDir(): string {
  return '';
}

export function runMake(_dir: string, _text: string, _args: readonly string[]): MakeRun {
  return { status: null, stdout: '', stderr: '' };
