cat > test/meta/make.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { chmodSync, existsSync, mkdtempSync, readdirSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { afterAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { fcMakeParams, fcParams } from '../support/fc-params.js';
import { gnuMakeVersion, makeDir, runMake } from '../support/make.js';

// The Make runner and the run count of the properties that spawn make (dinnerfile spec, T1.6).
// These tests need GNU Make on PATH and fail, not skip, without it (R9.3).

const dirs: string[] = [];
afterAll(() => {
  for (const dir of dirs) rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});
const freshDir = (): string => {
  const dir = makeDir();
  dirs.push(dir);
  return dir;
};

const TWO_RULES = ['.PHONY: dinner a.b', 'dinner: a.b', 'a.b:', "\t@echo 'hi' # a.b", ''].join('\n');

describe('R9.3 gnuMakeVersion', () => {
  it('the first line of make --version starts with GNU Make', () => {
    expect(gnuMakeVersion().startsWith('GNU Make')).toBe(true);
  });
  it('an empty PATH finds no make and throws a message that says D2 needs GNU Make on PATH', () => {
    expect(() => gnuMakeVersion('')).toThrowError(/D2 needs GNU Make on PATH/);
  });
  it('a make that is not GNU Make throws, and the message shows what it printed', () => {
    const bin = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'fake-make-'));
    dirs.push(bin);
    const script = join(bin, 'make');
    writeFileSync(script, '#!/bin/sh\necho "BSD make 1.0"\n');
    chmodSync(script, 0o755);
    expect(() => gnuMakeVersion(bin)).toThrowError(/D2 needs GNU Make on PATH.*BSD make 1\.0/);
  });
  it('a make that is GNU Make is accepted from another PATH', () => {
    const bin = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'fake-make-'));
    dirs.push(bin);
    const script = join(bin, 'make');
    writeFileSync(script, '#!/bin/sh\necho "GNU Make 9.9"\necho second line\n');
    chmodSync(script, 0o755);
    expect(gnuMakeVersion(bin)).toBe('GNU Make 9.9');
  });
});

describe('R10.4 makeDir and runMake', () => {
  it('makeDir gives a new empty directory under the system temp directory each time', () => {
    const a = freshDir();
    const b = freshDir();
    expect(a).not.toBe(b);
    expect(a.startsWith(tmpdir())).toBe(true);
    expect(existsSync(a)).toBe(true);
    expect(readdirSync(a)).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('runMake on a two-rule text prints the one echo line, status 0, empty stderr', () => {
    const run = runMake(freshDir(), TWO_RULES, ['-n', 'dinner']);
    expect(run).toEqual({ status: 0, stdout: "echo 'hi' # a.b\n", stderr: '' });
  });
  it('runMake writes the text as Dinnerfile and nothing else into the directory', () => {
    const dir = freshDir();
    runMake(dir, TWO_RULES, ['-n', 'dinner']);
    expect(readdirSync(dir)).toEqual(['Dinnerfile']);
  });
  it('runMake runs without -n when asked: the shell prints the word', () => {
    expect(runMake(freshDir(), TWO_RULES, ['dinner'])).toEqual({
      status: 0,
      stdout: 'hi\n',
      stderr: '',
    });
  });
  it('runMake reports a failing make: status 2 and a message on stderr', () => {
    const run = runMake(freshDir(), TWO_RULES, ['-n', 'no-such-target']);
    expect(run.status).toBe(2);
    expect(run.stdout).toBe('');
    expect(run.stderr).toContain('No rule to make target');
  });
  it('runMake passes only PATH: MAKEFLAGS in the environment does not change the result', () => {
    const saved = process.env.MAKEFLAGS;
    process.env.MAKEFLAGS = '-n';
    try {
      expect(runMake(freshDir(), TWO_RULES, ['dinner']).stdout).toBe('hi\n');
    } finally {
      if (saved === undefined) delete process.env.MAKEFLAGS;
      else process.env.MAKEFLAGS = saved;
    }
  });
  it('runMake throws, rather than returning, when the process cannot run', () => {
    expect(() => runMake(join(tmpdir(), 'dinnerfile-no-such-dir-xyz'), TWO_RULES, [])).toThrow();
  });
});

describe('R10.5 fcMakeParams', () => {
  const withEnv = (values: Record<string, string | undefined>, body: () => void): void => {
    const saved = Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(values).map((k) => [k, process.env[k]]));
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(values)) {
      if (v === undefined) delete process.env[k];
      else process.env[k] = v;
    }
    try {
      body();
    } finally {
      for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(saved)) {
        if (v === undefined) delete process.env[k];
        else process.env[k] = v;
      }
    }
  };

  it('is 50 runs by default', () => {
    withEnv({ FC_MAKE_RUNS: undefined, FC_NUM_RUNS: undefined }, () => {
      expect(fcMakeParams()).toEqual({ numRuns: 50 });
    });
  });
  it('FC_MAKE_RUNS=7 gives 7', () => {
    withEnv({ FC_MAKE_RUNS: '7' }, () => {
      expect(fcMakeParams()).toEqual({ numRuns: 7 });
    });
  });
  it.each(['0', '-3', '2.5', 'x', ''])('FC_MAKE_RUNS=%j is not a positive integer: 50', (value) => {
    withEnv({ FC_MAKE_RUNS: value }, () => {
      expect(fcMakeParams()).toEqual({ numRuns: 50 });
    });
  });
  it('FC_NUM_RUNS does not change it, and FC_MAKE_RUNS does not change fcParams', () => {
    withEnv({ FC_MAKE_RUNS: '7', FC_NUM_RUNS: '9' }, () => {
      expect(fcMakeParams()).toEqual({ numRuns: 7 });
      expect(fcParams()).toEqual({ numRuns: 9 });
    });
    withEnv({ FC_MAKE_RUNS: undefined, FC_NUM_RUNS: '9' }, () => {
      expect(fcMakeParams()).toEqual({ numRuns: 50 });
    });
  });
});

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > test/support/make.ts <<'EOF'
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdtempSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';

// A GNU Make runner for the Dinnerfile tests (dinnerfile design, "Make runner"). Only `PATH`
// reaches make, so `MAKEFLAGS`, `MAKEFILES` or `SHELL` in a developer's environment cannot
// change a result. A missing or foreign make throws: D2 and P-DF7 fail, not skip (R9.3).

export interface MakeRun {
  status: number | null;
  stdout: string;
  stderr: string;
}

const TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;

/**
 * The first line of `make --version`. Throws when make is missing or the line does not start
 * with "GNU Make", with a message saying that D2 needs GNU Make on PATH. `path` is the value
 * of `PATH` to search; the default is the current one.
 */
export function gnuMakeVersion(path: string = process.env.PATH ?? ''): string {
  const result = spawnSync('make', ['--version'], {
    env: { PATH: path },
    encoding: 'utf8',
    timeout: TIMEOUT_MS,
  });
  if (result.error !== undefined) {
    throw new Error(
      `D2 needs GNU Make on PATH, but make could not be run (${result.error.message}). Install GNU Make 3.81 or later.`,
    );
  }
  const first = result.stdout.split('\n')[0] ?? '';
  if (!first.startsWith('GNU Make')) {
    throw new Error(
      `D2 needs GNU Make on PATH, but "make --version" printed "${first}". Install GNU Make 3.81 or later.`,
    );
  }
  return first;
}

/** A fresh directory under os.tmpdir(); callers remove it in afterAll. */
export function makeDir(): string {
  return mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'dinnerfile-'));
}

let checked = false;

/** Writes dir/Dinnerfile and runs `make -f Dinnerfile ...args` there with env { PATH } only. */
export function runMake(dir: string, text: string, args: readonly string[]): MakeRun {
  if (!checked) {
    gnuMakeVersion();
    checked = true;
  }
  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'Dinnerfile'), text, 'utf8');
  const result = spawnSync('make', ['-f', 'Dinnerfile', ...args], {
    cwd: dir,
    env: { PATH: process.env.PATH ?? '' },
    encoding: 'utf8',
    timeout: TIMEOUT_MS,
  });
  if (result.error !== undefined) throw result.error;
  return { status: result.status, stdout: result.stdout, stderr: result.stderr };
}

  return { numRuns };
}""")

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && sed -i '' 's/^const DINNERFILE_HEADER: readonly string\[\]/export const DINNERFILE_HEADER: readonly string[]/' test/support/fixtures.ts && grep -n "DINNERFILE_HEADER" test/support/fixtures.ts | head -2 && cat > test/core/dinnerfile.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { toDinnerfile } from '../../src/core/dinnerfile.js';
import { err, ok } from '../../src/core/result.js';
import type { Plan, Step } from '../../src/core/schema.js';
import {
  DINNERFILE_HEADER,
  HOME_KITCHEN,
  SUNDAY_ROAST,
  SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE,
  WORST_CASE,
  WORST_CASE_DINNERFILE,
} from '../support/fixtures.js';

// Dinnerfile spec, T2.1: export. Expected texts are written out by hand from the design.

const step = (id: string, over: Partial<Step> = {}): Step => ({
  id,
  dish: id.split('.')[0] ?? '',
  title: id,
  minutes: 1,
  appliance: 'none',
  handsOn: true,
  after: [],
  holdMin: null,
  ...over,
});

/** The expected text: the four header lines, a blank line, then `body`, one LF at the end. */
const withHeader = (body: readonly string[]): string =>
  [...DINNERFILE_HEADER, '', ...body].join('\n') + '\n';

describe('R1.1 toDinnerfile on the hand-worked plans', () => {
  it('SUNDAY_ROAST is SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE, character for character', () => {
    expect(toDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST)).toEqual(ok(SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE));
  });
  it('WORST_CASE is WORST_CASE_DINNERFILE, character for character', () => {
    expect(toDinnerfile(WORST_CASE)).toEqual(ok(WORST_CASE_DINNERFILE));
  });
  it('options {} and no options are the same', () => {
    expect(toDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST, {})).toEqual(toDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST));
  });
});

describe('R5.2 the kitchen and serve lines', () => {
  const lines = (text: string): string[] => text.split('\n');
  const plain = lines(SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE);

  it('a kitchen and a serving time are lines 7 and 8, directly after the # plan line', () => {
    const result = toDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST, { kitchen: HOME_KITCHEN, serve: '19:00' });
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    const out = lines(result.ok ? result.value : '');
    expect(out[5]).toBe('# plan "Sunday roast"');
    expect(out[6]).toBe('# kitchen burners=4 ovens=1 cooks=1');
    expect(out[7]).toBe('# serve 19:00');
    expect([...out.slice(0, 6), ...out.slice(8)]).toEqual(plain);
  });
  it('a kitchen alone is line 7, a serving time alone is line 7', () => {
    const kitchen = toDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST, { kitchen: { burners: 0, ovens: 4, cooks: 6 } });
    const serve = toDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST, { serve: '00:00' });
    expect(kitchen.ok && lines(kitchen.value)[6]).toBe('# kitchen burners=0 ovens=4 cooks=6');
    expect(serve.ok && lines(serve.value)[6]).toBe('# serve 00:00');
    expect(kitchen.ok && lines(kitchen.value).length).toBe(plain.length + 1);
    expect(serve.ok && lines(serve.value).length).toBe(plain.length + 1);
  });
  it('R5.3: without the two lines the text is the text without options', () => {
    const result = toDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST, { kitchen: HOME_KITCHEN, serve: '19:00' });
    const stripped = lines(result.ok ? result.value : '')
      .filter((l) => !l.startsWith('# kitchen ') && !l.startsWith('# serve '))
      .join('\n');
    expect(stripped).toBe(SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE);
  });
  it('only the keys kitchen and serve of the options are read', () => {
    const options = { serve: '19:00', note: 'x', kitchen: undefined } as unknown as Parameters<
      typeof toDinnerfile
    >[1];
    const result = toDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST, options);
    expect(result.ok && lines(result.value).filter((l) => l.startsWith('# '))[5]).toBe(
      '# serve 19:00',
    );
    expect(result.ok && result.value.includes('note')).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('R1.3 order does not matter', () => {
  it('a hand-shuffled SUNDAY_ROAST (steps and dishes reversed) gives the same text', () => {
    const shuffled: Plan = {
      ...SUNDAY_ROAST,
      dishes: [...SUNDAY_ROAST.dishes].reverse(),
      steps: [...SUNDAY_ROAST.steps].reverse(),
    };
    expect(toDinnerfile(shuffled)).toEqual(ok(SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE));
  });
  it('after lists are written sorted, once each: a.z waits on a.x a.y however it was listed', () => {
    const plan: Plan = {
      title: 'Order',
      dishes: [{ id: 'a', name: 'A' }],
      steps: [
        step('a.z', { after: ['a.y', 'a.x', 'a.y'], title: 'Z' }),
        step('a.y', { title: 'Y' }),
        step('a.x', { title: 'X' }),
      ],
    };
    const result = toDinnerfile(plan);
    expect(result).toEqual(
      ok(
        withHeader([
          '# plan "Order"',
          '# dish a "A"',
          '',
          '.PHONY: dinner a a.x a.y a.z',
          'dinner: a.z',
          '',
          'a: a.z',
          '',
          '# step dish=a minutes=1 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="X"',
          'a.x:',
          "\t@echo 'X' # a.x",
          '',
          '# step dish=a minutes=1 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="Y"',
          'a.y:',
          "\t@echo 'Y' # a.y",
          '',
          '# step dish=a minutes=1 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="Z"',
          'a.z: a.x a.y',
          "\t@echo 'Z' # a.z",
        ]),
      ),
    );
  });
});

describe('R1.7, R4.2 dish rules', () => {
  it('a dish with no steps gets the rule "roast:" with no prerequisites, and is in .PHONY', () => {
    const plan: Plan = {
      title: 'Two dishes',
      dishes: [
        { id: 'roast', name: 'Roast' },
        { id: 'greens', name: 'Greens' },
      ],
      steps: [step('roast.cook', { title: 'Roast it', minutes: 30, appliance: 'oven', holdMin: 10, handsOn: false })],
    };
    expect(toDinnerfile(plan)).toEqual(
      ok(
        withHeader([
          '# plan "Two dishes"',
          '# dish greens "Greens"',
          '# dish roast "Roast"',
          '',
          '.PHONY: dinner greens roast roast.cook',
          'dinner: roast.cook',
          '',
          'greens:',
          'roast: roast.cook',
          '',
          '# step dish=roast minutes=30 appliance=oven handsOn=false hold=10 title="Roast it"',
          'roast.cook:',
          "\t@echo 'Roast it' # roast.cook",
        ]),
      ),
    );
  });
  it('a dish called dinner gets the fixed comment line and no rule; dinner stays the whole plan', () => {
    const plan: Plan = {
      title: 'Dinner dish',
      dishes: [{ id: 'dinner', name: 'Dinner' }],
      steps: [step('dinner.serve', { title: 'Serve' })],
    };
    expect(toDinnerfile(plan)).toEqual(
      ok(
        withHeader([
          '# plan "Dinner dish"',
          '# dish dinner "Dinner"',
          '',
          '.PHONY: dinner dinner.serve',
          'dinner: dinner.serve',
          '',
          '# No target for dish dinner: "dinner" makes the whole plan.',
          '',
          '# step dish=dinner minutes=1 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="Serve"',
          'dinner.serve:',
          "\t@echo 'Serve' # dinner.serve",
        ]),
      ),
    );
  });
  it('a dish rule lists only the last steps of the dish: chicken.roast is not one', () => {
    const text = toDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST);
    const lines = (text.ok ? text.value : '').split('\n');
    expect(lines).toContain('chicken: chicken.carve');
    expect(lines).not.toContain('chicken: chicken.carve chicken.roast');
    expect(lines.filter((l) => /^(chicken|gravy|greens):/.test(l)).length).toBe(3);
  });
  it('a dish with two last steps lists both, sorted, and steps of other dishes do not count', () => {
    const plan: Plan = {
      title: 'Two ends',
      dishes: [
        { id: 'a', name: 'A' },
        { id: 'b', name: 'B' },
      ],
      steps: [
        step('a.one', { title: 'One' }),
        step('a.two', { title: 'Two', after: ['a.one'] }),
        step('a.three', { title: 'Three', after: ['a.one'] }),
        // b.use waits on a.three from another dish; a.three is still a last step of a.
        step('b.use', { title: 'Use', after: ['a.three'] }),
      ],
    };
    const result = toDinnerfile(plan);
    const lines = (result.ok ? result.value : '').split('\n');
    expect(lines).toContain('a: a.three a.two');
    expect(lines).toContain('b: b.use');
    expect(lines).toContain('dinner: a.two b.use');
  });
});

describe('R1.2 plans the export accepts although the sanity rules would not', () => {
  it('a plan with a cycle exports: dinner has no prerequisites and the dish rule is empty', () => {
    const plan: Plan = {
      title: 'Cycle',
      dishes: [{ id: 'a', name: 'A' }],
      steps: [
        step('a.x', { title: 'X', after: ['a.y'] }),
        step('a.y', { title: 'Y', after: ['a.x'] }),
      ],
    };
    expect(toDinnerfile(plan)).toEqual(
      ok(
        withHeader([
          '# plan "Cycle"',
          '# dish a "A"',
          '',
          '.PHONY: dinner a a.x a.y',
          'dinner:',
          '',
          'a:',
          '',
          '# step dish=a minutes=1 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="X"',
          'a.x: a.y',
          "\t@echo 'X' # a.x",
          '',
          '# step dish=a minutes=1 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="Y"',
          'a.y: a.x',
          "\t@echo 'Y' # a.y",
        ]),
      ),
    );
  });
  it('an unknown prerequisite is written as it is', () => {
    const plan: Plan = {
      title: 'Unknown',
      dishes: [{ id: 'a', name: 'A' }],
      steps: [step('a.x', { title: 'X', after: ['a.missing'] })],
    };
    const result = toDinnerfile(plan);
    expect(result.ok && result.value.split('\n')).toContain('a.x: a.missing');
  });
});

describe('R1.2 plans and options that fail their schemas', () => {
  it('a step id with a space gives exactly the issue parsePlan gives, and no text', () => {
    const bad = {
      ...SUNDAY_ROAST,
      steps: SUNDAY_ROAST.steps.map((s) => (s.id === 'greens.toss' ? { ...s, id: 'a b' } : s)),
    };
    expect(toDinnerfile(bad)).toEqual(
      err([
        {
          severity: 'error',
          code: 'invalid_field',
          field: 'steps[0].id',
          message:
            'Step 1: the id must look like chicken.roast: lowercase letters, digits and hyphens around one dot, up to 80 characters.',
        },
      ]),
    );
  });
  it("serve '24:00' gives exactly the serve issue", () => {
    expect(toDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST, { serve: '24:00' })).toEqual(
      err([
        {
          severity: 'error',
          code: 'invalid_field',
          field: 'serve',
          message: 'The serving time must be a 24-hour time from 00:00 to 23:59, like 19:00.',
        },
      ]),
    );
  });
  it('kitchen.cooks 0 gives exactly the issue parseKitchen gives', () => {
    expect(toDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST, { kitchen: { burners: 4, ovens: 1, cooks: 0 } })).toEqual(
      err([
        {
          severity: 'error',
          code: 'invalid_field',
          field: 'cooks',
          message: 'The kitchen: cooks must be a whole number from 1 to 6.',
        },
      ]),
    );
  });
  it('a bad plan, kitchen and serving time together give all three, in sortIssues order', () => {
    const bad = { ...SUNDAY_ROAST, title: '' };
    const result = toDinnerfile(bad, {
      kitchen: { burners: 4, ovens: 1, cooks: 0 },
      serve: '7:00',
    });
    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    const fields = result.ok ? [] : result.error.map((i) => i.code === 'invalid_field' && i.field);
    expect(fields.slice().sort()).toEqual(['cooks', 'serve', 'title']);
    expect(fields).toEqual(['title', 'cooks', 'serve']);
  });
  it.each([null, undefined, 42, 'text', [], {}])('%j as a plan is err with issues, never a throw', (value) => {
    const result = toDinnerfile(value as unknown as Plan);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(result.ok ? 0 : result.error.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && cat > test/core/dinnerfile-make.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { readdirSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { afterAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { toDinnerfile } from '../../src/core/dinnerfile.js';
import type { Plan } from '../../src/core/schema.js';
import {
  MEZZE,
  SUNDAY_ROAST,
  SUNDAY_ROAST_MAKE_DINNER,
  SUNDAY_ROAST_MAKE_GRAVY,
  TIGHT_OVEN,
  WORST_CASE,
  WORST_CASE_MAKE_DINNER,
} from '../support/fixtures.js';
import { gnuMakeVersion, makeDir, runMake } from '../support/make.js';
import { decodeEchoLine, oracleEchoText, respectsPrecedence } from '../support/oracles.js';

// Dinnerfile spec, T2.2: GNU Make on the output of toDinnerfile (not on the fixtures). These
// tests need GNU Make on PATH and fail, not skip, without it (R9.3).

const dirs: string[] = [];
afterAll(() => {
  for (const dir of dirs) rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});
const freshDir = (): string => {
  const dir = makeDir();
  dirs.push(dir);
  return dir;
};

const exported = (plan: Plan): string => {
  const result = toDinnerfile(plan);
  if (!result.ok) throw new Error('the export failed');
  return result.value;
};

/** The lines Make printed, without the final LF. */
const printed = (stdout: string): string[] => stdout.replace(/\n$/, '').split('\n');

describe('R9.3 GNU Make is on PATH', () => {
  it('make --version starts with GNU Make', () => {
    expect(gnuMakeVersion().startsWith('GNU Make')).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('R3.5, R9.1, R9.2 make -n on the export of SUNDAY_ROAST', () => {
  const text = exported(SUNDAY_ROAST);

  it('make -n dinner prints exactly SUNDAY_ROAST_MAKE_DINNER, exit 0, empty stderr', () => {
    const run = runMake(freshDir(), text, ['-n', 'dinner']);
    expect(run.status).toBe(0);
    expect(run.stderr).toBe('');
    expect(printed(run.stdout)).toEqual(SUNDAY_ROAST_MAKE_DINNER);
  });
  it('make -n gravy prints exactly SUNDAY_ROAST_MAKE_GRAVY', () => {
    const run = runMake(freshDir(), text, ['-n', 'gravy']);
    expect(run.status).toBe(0);
    expect(run.stderr).toBe('');
    expect(printed(run.stdout)).toEqual(SUNDAY_ROAST_MAKE_GRAVY);
  });
  it('R1.5: make -n with no goal prints the same as make -n dinner', () => {
    const dir = freshDir();
    const noGoal = runMake(dir, text, ['-n']);
    const dinner = runMake(dir, text, ['-n', 'dinner']);
    expect(noGoal.status).toBe(0);
    expect(noGoal.stdout).toBe(dinner.stdout);
    expect(printed(noGoal.stdout)).toEqual(SUNDAY_ROAST_MAKE_DINNER);
  });
});

describe('R3.5, R3.6, R4.2 make on the export of WORST_CASE', () => {
  const text = exported(WORST_CASE);

  it('make -n dinner prints exactly WORST_CASE_MAKE_DINNER, exit 0, empty stderr', () => {
    const run = runMake(freshDir(), text, ['-n', 'dinner']);
    expect(run.status).toBe(0);
    expect(run.stderr).toBe('');
    expect(printed(run.stdout)).toEqual(WORST_CASE_MAKE_DINNER);
  });
  it('make dinner without -n exits 0 and leaves only the Dinnerfile: nothing ran but echo', () => {
    const dir = freshDir();
    const run = runMake(dir, text, ['dinner']);
    expect(run.status).toBe(0);
    expect(run.stderr).toBe('');
    expect(readdirSync(dir)).toEqual(['Dinnerfile']);
  });
  it('the dish called dinner has no rule of its own: make -n export prints its two steps', () => {
    const run = runMake(freshDir(), text, ['-n', 'export']);
    expect(run.status).toBe(0);
    expect(printed(run.stdout).map((l) => decodeEchoLine(l, 'printed')?.stepId)).toEqual([
      '--.--',
      'export.x',
    ]);
  });
});

describe.each([
  ['MEZZE', MEZZE],
  ['TIGHT_OVEN', TIGHT_OVEN],
])('D2 checks on the export of %s', (_name, plan) => {
  it('make -n dinner exits 0 with empty stderr, prints every step once, in an order that works', () => {
    const run = runMake(freshDir(), exported(plan), ['-n', 'dinner']);
    expect(run.status).toBe(0);
    expect(run.stderr).toBe('');
    const decoded = printed(run.stdout).map((l) => decodeEchoLine(l, 'printed'));
    expect(decoded.every((d) => d !== null)).toBe(true);
    const ids = decoded.map((d) => d?.stepId ?? '');
    expect(ids.slice().sort()).toEqual(plan.steps.map((s) => s.id).sort());
    expect(respectsPrecedence(plan, ids)).toBe(true);
    for (const d of decoded) {
      const title = plan.steps.find((s) => s.id === d?.stepId)?.title ?? '';
      expect(d?.text).toBe(oracleEchoText(title));
    }
  });
});

import fc from 'fast-check';
import { expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { toDinnerfile } from '../../src/core/dinnerfile.js';
import type { DinnerfileOptions } from '../../src/core/dinnerfile.js';
import type { Plan } from '../../src/core/schema.js';
import { arbDinnerfileOptions, arbPlan, arbShuffledPlan } from '../support/arbitraries.js';
import { fcParams } from '../support/fc-params.js';
import {
  decodeEchoLine,
  oracleDinnerfile,
  oracleEchoText,
  oracleMakeLines,
} from '../support/oracles.js';

// Dinnerfile properties that need no make (dinnerfile design, "Correctness properties"). Titles
// start with the id and a space. Plans are default-sized and large (12 dishes, 80 steps), with
// hostile titles and details, a dish called dinner and directive-word dish ids from the
// extended generators.

const TIMEOUT_MS = 60_000;

/** Runs `check` on valid plans of both sizes with options that pass their schemas. */
const forPlans = (check: (plan: Plan, options: DinnerfileOptions) => void): void => {
  for (const large of [false, true]) {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(arbPlan(large ? { large: true } : {}), arbDinnerfileOptions(), check),
      fcParams(),
    );
  }
};

/** The export of a plan the generators made valid; a failed export fails the property. */
const exportOf = (plan: Plan, options?: DinnerfileOptions): string => {
  const result = toDinnerfile(plan, options);
  expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
  return result.ok ? result.value : '';
};

it(
  'P-DF2 Make lines: every line is a form Make reads as intended, the text ends in one LF, dinner is the first goal',
  () => {
    forPlans((plan, options) => {
      const text = exportOf(plan, options);
      const lines = text.split('\n');
      expect(oracleMakeLines(text).filter((kind) => kind === 'other')).toEqual([]);
      expect(text.endsWith('\n')).toBe(true);
      expect(text.endsWith('\n\n')).toBe(false);
      expect(text.includes('\r')).toBe(false);
      expect(lines.filter((l) => l.endsWith('\\') || /[ \t]$/.test(l))).toEqual([]);
      expect(text.isWellFormed()).toBe(true);
      const firstGoal = lines.find((l) => /^[^#\t]/.test(l) && !l.startsWith('.PHONY:'));
      expect(firstGoal?.startsWith('dinner:')).toBe(true);
      const recipes = lines.filter((l) => l.startsWith('\t'));
      expect(recipes.filter((l) => l.includes('$(') || l.includes('${'))).toEqual([]);
      expect(recipes.length).toBe(plan.steps.length);
    });
  },
  TIMEOUT_MS,
);

it(
  'P-DF6 echo: the recipe line of each step decodes to its id and the display form of its title',
  () => {
    forPlans((plan, options) => {
      const lines = exportOf(plan, options).split('\n');
      for (const step of plan.steps) {
        const rule = lines.findIndex((l) => l.startsWith(`${step.id}:`));
        expect(rule).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
        expect(decodeEchoLine(lines[rule + 1] ?? '', 'file')).toEqual({
          text: oracleEchoText(step.title),
          stepId: step.id,
        });
      }
    });
  },
  TIMEOUT_MS,
);

it(
  'P-DF9 order-free: any order of steps, dishes and after lists gives the same text',
  () => {
    for (const large of [false, true]) {
      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          arbPlan(large ? { large: true } : {}).chain((plan) =>
            fc.tuple(fc.constant(plan), arbShuffledPlan(plan)),
          ),
          ([plan, shuffled]) => {
            expect(exportOf(shuffled)).toBe(exportOf(plan));
          },
        ),
        fcParams(),
      );
    }
  },
  TIMEOUT_MS,
);

it(
  'P-DF10 layout: the export equals the oracle layout, line for line',
  () => {
    forPlans((plan, options) => {
      expect(exportOf(plan, options)).toBe(oracleDinnerfile(plan, options));
    });
  },
  TIMEOUT_MS,
);
EOF

cat > test/core/dinnerfile-make.pbt.ts <<'EOF'
import { rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import fc from 'fast-check';
import { afterAll, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { toDinnerfile } from '../../src/core/dinnerfile.js';
import type { DinnerfileOptions } from '../../src/core/dinnerfile.js';
import type { Plan } from '../../src/core/schema.js';
import { arbDinnerfileOptions, arbPlan } from '../support/arbitraries.js';
import { fcMakeParams } from '../support/fc-params.js';
import { makeDir, runMake } from '../support/make.js';
import {
  decodeEchoLine,
  oracleEchoText,
  oracleNeededSteps,
  respectsPrecedence,
} from '../support/oracles.js';

// D2 and P-DF7 spawn GNU Make (about 11 ms a run), so they use fcMakeParams(): 50 runs by
// default, FC_MAKE_RUNS to change (dinnerfile design, conflict C1). They need GNU Make on PATH
// and fail, not skip, without it (R9.3). One temporary directory for the file.

const TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000;

const dir = makeDir();
afterAll(() => {
  rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

const exportOf = (plan: Plan, options: DinnerfileOptions): string => {
  const result = toDinnerfile(plan, options);
  expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
  return result.ok ? result.value : '';
};

/** What `make -n` printed, as step ids and texts; fails the property on a line of another form. */
const decodePrinted = (stdout: string): { stepId: string; text: string }[] => {
  expect(stdout === '' || stdout.endsWith('\n')).toBe(true);
  const lines = stdout === '' ? [] : stdout.replace(/\n$/, '').split('\n');
  return lines.map((line) => {
    const decoded = decodeEchoLine(line, 'printed');
    expect(decoded).not.toBeNull();
    return decoded ?? { stepId: '', text: '' };
  });
};

it(
  'D2 make agrees: make -n dinner exits 0 with no stderr and prints every step once, after its prerequisites',
  () => {
    for (const large of [false, true]) {
      fc.assert(
        fc.property(arbPlan(large ? { large: true } : {}), arbDinnerfileOptions(), (plan, options) => {
          const run = runMake(dir, exportOf(plan, options), ['-n', 'dinner']);
          expect(run.status).toBe(0);
          expect(run.stderr).toBe('');
          const printed = decodePrinted(run.stdout);
          const ids = printed.map((p) => p.stepId);
          expect(ids.slice().sort()).toEqual(plan.steps.map((s) => s.id).sort());
          expect(respectsPrecedence(plan, ids)).toBe(true);
          for (const p of printed) {
            const title = plan.steps.find((s) => s.id === p.stepId)?.title ?? '';
            expect(p.text).toBe(oracleEchoText(title));
          }
        }),
        fcMakeParams(),
      );
    }
  },
  TIMEOUT_MS,
);

it(
  'P-DF7 dish target: make -n <dish> prints exactly the steps that dish needs, once each, in an order that works',
  () => {
    for (const large of [false, true]) {
      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          arbPlan(large ? { large: true } : {}),
          fc.nat(),
          arbDinnerfileOptions(),
          (plan, pick, options) => {
            // A dish called dinner has no rule of its own (R4.2); there is nothing to run for it.
            const dishes = plan.dishes.filter((d) => d.id !== 'dinner');
            if (dishes.length === 0) return;
            const dish = dishes[pick % dishes.length]?.id ?? '';
            const run = runMake(dir, exportOf(plan, options), ['-n', dish]);
            expect(run.status).toBe(0);
            expect(run.stderr).toBe('');
            const ids = decodePrinted(run.stdout).map((p) => p.stepId);
            expect(ids.slice().sort()).toEqual(oracleNeededSteps(plan, dish));
            const position = new Map(ids.map((id, i) => [id, i]));
            for (const step of plan.steps) {
              const at = position.get(step.id);
              if (at === undefined) continue;
              for (const before of step.after) {
                expect(position.get(before) ?? Infinity).toBeLessThan(at);
              }
            }
          },
        ),
        fcMakeParams(),
      );
    }
  },
  TIMEOUT_MS,
);
EOF

[tool:execute] $ cd ~/hackathons-2026/kiroween/makedinner && python3 - <<'PYEOF'
p='src/core/dinnerfile.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("""import { err } from './result.js';
import type { Result } from './result.js';
import type { Issue, Kitchen, Plan, ServeTime } from './schema.js';
""","""import { terminalSteps } from './graph.js';
import { err, ok } from './result.js';
import type { Result } from './result.js';
import { parseKitchen, parsePlan, sortIssues } from './sanity.js';
import { canonicalPlan, ServeTimeSchema } from './schema.js';
import type { Issue, Kitchen, Plan, ServeTime } from './schema.js';
""",1)
i=s.index("/** The Dinnerfile text of a plan.")
s=s[:i]+r'''// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Export

const HEADER: readonly string[] = [
  '# Dinnerfile written by Make Dinner. GNU Make reads it:',
  '#   make -n -f Dinnerfile dinner    prints every step in an order that works',
  '#   make -n -f Dinnerfile <dish>    prints the steps one dish needs',
  '# The "# plan", "# dish", "# step", "# kitchen" and "# serve" lines hold the plan.',
];

const NO_DINNER_DISH_RULE = '# No target for dish dinner: "dinner" makes the whole plan.';

const SERVE_ISSUE_MESSAGE =
  'The serving time must be a 24-hour time from 00:00 to 23:59, like 19:00.';

const isLoneSurrogate = (cp: number): boolean => cp >= 0xd800 && cp <= 0xdfff;
const isControl = (cp: number): boolean => cp <= 0x1f || (cp >= 0x7f && cp <= 0x9f);
const isBidiControl = (cp: number): boolean =>
  cp === 0x61c ||
  cp === 0x200e ||
  cp === 0x200f ||
  (cp >= 0x202a && cp <= 0x202e) ||
  (cp >= 0x2066 && cp <= 0x2069);

const SHORT_ESCAPES = new Map<number, string>([
  [0x22, '\\"'],
  [0x5c, '\\\\'],
  [0x08, '\\b'],
  [0x0c, '\\f'],
  [0x0a, '\\n'],
  [0x0d, '\\r'],
  [0x09, '\\t'],
]);

const unicodeEscape = (cp: number): string => `\\u${cp.toString(16).padStart(4, '0')}`;

/**
 * Text in a metadata line: a JSON string that reads back unit for unit. Line breaks, control
 * and bidirectional code points, U+2028, U+2029, U+FEFF and lone surrogates are escaped, so the
 * line holds none of them and ends with the closing quote.
 */
function enc(text: string): string {
  let out = '"';
  for (const ch of text) {
    const cp = ch.codePointAt(0) ?? 0;
    const short = SHORT_ESCAPES.get(cp);
    if (short !== undefined) out += short;
    else if (
      isControl(cp) ||
      cp === 0x2028 ||
      cp === 0x2029 ||
      isBidiControl(cp) ||
      cp === 0xfeff ||
      isLoneSurrogate(cp)
    ) {
      out += unicodeEscape(cp);
    } else out += ch;
  }
  return `${out}"`;
}

/**
 * The argument of the `@echo` line: the title's display form (one line, nothing that reorders
 * or hides text), quoted for the shell (`'` as `'\\''`) and for Make (`$` as `$$`, with `''`
 * after it before `(` or `{`, so the line never holds `$(` or `${`).
 */
function echoArg(title: string): string {
  const display: string[] = [];
  for (const ch of title) {
    const cp = ch.codePointAt(0) ?? 0;
    if (isControl(cp) || cp === 0x2028 || cp === 0x2029) display.push(' ');
    else if (isBidiControl(cp) || cp === 0xfeff || isLoneSurrogate(cp)) display.push('\ufffd');
    else display.push(ch);
  }
  let out = '';
  display.forEach((ch, i) => {
    if (ch === "'") out += "'\\''";
    else if (ch === '$') {
      const next = display[i + 1];
      out += next === '(' || next === '{' ? "$$''" : '$$';
    } else out += ch;
  });
  return out;
}

const rule = (target: string, prerequisites: readonly string[]): string =>
  prerequisites.length === 0 ? `${target}:` : `${target}: ${prerequisites.join(' ')}`;

/** Per dish id, the steps of that dish no other step of the same dish waits on, sorted. */
function lastSteps(plan: Plan, dishId: string): string[] {
  const ofDish = plan.steps.filter((s) => s.dish === dishId);
  const waitedOn = new Set(ofDish.flatMap((s) => s.after));
  const last = new Set(ofDish.filter((s) => !waitedOn.has(s.id)).map((s) => s.id));
  return [...last]; // plan.steps is in id order, so this is too
}

/** The Dinnerfile text of a plan. err when the plan or the options fail their schemas. */
export function toDinnerfile(plan: Plan, options?: DinnerfileOptions): Result<string, Issue[]> {
  const issues: Issue[] = [];
  const parsed = parsePlan(plan);
  if (!parsed.ok) issues.push(...parsed.error);

  // Only the keys kitchen and serve are read, and an undefined value means absent.
  const given: Record<string, unknown> =
    typeof options === 'object' && options !== null ? { ...options } : {};
  const kitchenInput = given['kitchen'];
  const serveInput = given['serve'];
  let kitchen: Kitchen | undefined;
  if (kitchenInput !== undefined) {
    const k = parseKitchen(kitchenInput);
    if (k.ok) kitchen = k.value;
    else issues.push(...k.error);
  }
  let serve: ServeTime | undefined;
  if (serveInput !== undefined) {
    const t = ServeTimeSchema.safeParse(serveInput);
    if (t.success) serve = t.data;
    else {
      issues.push({
        severity: 'error',
        code: 'invalid_field',
        field: 'serve',
        message: SERVE_ISSUE_MESSAGE,
      });
    }
  }
  if (!parsed.ok || issues.length > 0) return err(sortIssues(issues));

  const p = canonicalPlan(parsed.value);
  const dishIds = [...new Set(p.dishes.map((d) => d.id))];
  const lines: string[] = [...HEADER, '', `# plan ${enc(p.title)}`];
  if (kitchen !== undefined) {
    lines.push(
      `# kitchen burners=${kitchen.burners} ovens=${kitchen.ovens} cooks=${kitchen.cooks}`,
    );
  }
  if (serve !== undefined) lines.push(`# serve ${serve}`);
  for (const dish of p.dishes) lines.push(`# dish ${dish.id} ${enc(dish.name)}`);
  lines.push('');
  lines.push(
    `.PHONY: ${['dinner', ...dishIds.filter((id) => id !== 'dinner'), ...p.steps.map((s) => s.id)].join(' ')}`,
  );
  lines.push(rule('dinner', terminalSteps(p)));
  lines.push('');
  for (const id of dishIds) {
    lines.push(id === 'dinner' ? NO_DINNER_DISH_RULE : rule(id, lastSteps(p, id)));
  }
  for (const step of p.steps) {
    const detail = step.detail === undefined ? '' : ` detail=${enc(step.detail)}`;
    lines.push(
      '',
      `# step dish=${step.dish} minutes=${step.minutes} appliance=${step.appliance} handsOn=${step.handsOn} hold=${step.holdMin ?? 'keeps'} title=${enc(step.title)}${detail}`,
      rule(step.id, step.after),
      `\t@echo '${echoArg(step.title)}' # ${step.id}`,
    );
  }
  return ok(`${lines.join('\n')}\n`);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Import

/** Reads a Dinnerfile back. Never throws. */
export function fromDinnerfile(_text: string): Result<DinnerfileImport, DinnerfileError[]> {
  return err([]);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// T3.1: import, accepted files. The variants are the hand-written ones of the design outline,
// each made from SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE by a change written out in the test.

const SUNDAY_RESULT = ok({ plan: canonicalPlan(SUNDAY_ROAST), options: {} });

/** The blank-line separated sections of SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE: header, plan lines, .PHONY and
 *  dinner, dish rules, then the ten step blocks. */
const sections = SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE.trimEnd().split('\n\n');
const baseLines = SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE.split('\n');

describe('R6.3 fromDinnerfile reads the exports back', () => {
  it('SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE gives the canonical plan and no options', () => {
    expect(fromDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE)).toStrictEqual(SUNDAY_RESULT);
  });
  it('the result is not shared with the text: the plan has the ten steps and three dishes', () => {
    const result = fromDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE);
    expect(result.ok && result.value.plan.steps.length).toBe(10);
    expect(result.ok && result.value.plan.dishes.map((d) => d.id)).toEqual([
      'chicken',
      'gravy',
      'greens',
    ]);
  });
  it('R2.4 WORST_CASE_DINNERFILE gives canonicalPlan(WORST_CASE) with every hostile character back', () => {
    const result = fromDinnerfile(WORST_CASE_DINNERFILE);
    expect(result).toStrictEqual(ok({ plan: canonicalPlan(WORST_CASE), options: {} }));
    const titles = result.ok ? result.value.plan.steps.map((s) => s.title) : [];
    const exportX = titles[2] ?? '';
    expect(sections.length).toBe(14);
    expect(exportX.includes('\ud800 \\')).toBe(true);
    expect(exportX.includes('\t')).toBe(true);
    expect(exportX.includes('\n')).toBe(true);
    expect(exportX.startsWith(' It')).toBe(true);
    expect(titles[1]).toBe('Line\r\nbreak\u0000 and \u2028end');
    const dishes = result.ok ? result.value.plan.dishes.map((d) => d.name) : [];
    expect(dishes).toEqual(['D\u00e9finir', 'Dinner itself', 'Export  ']);
    const detail = result.ok ? result.value.plan.steps[2]?.detail : undefined;
    expect(detail).toBe('Line one\nLine two "quoted" \\c');
  });
});

describe('R6.6 changes of form that do not change the result', () => {
  const variants: [string, string][] = [
    ['CRLF line endings', SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE.replaceAll('\n', '\r\n')],
    ['a leading BOM', `\ufeff${SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE}`],
    ['BOM and CRLF', `\ufeff${SUNDAY_ROAST_DINNERFILE.replaceAll('\n', '\r\n')}`],
    [
      'spaces and tabs at the end of every line',
      baseLines.map((l, i) => l + (i % 2 === 0 ? '   ' : '\t ')).join('\n'),
    ],
    ['no header comments', baseLines.slice(5).join('\n')],
    [
      'no .PHONY rule',
      baseLines.filter((l) => !l.startsWith('.PHONY:')).join('\n'),
    ],
    ['a repeated .PHONY rule', baseLines.flatMap((l) => (l.startsWith('.PHONY:') ? [l, l] : [l])).join('\n')],
    [
      'no recipe lines',
      baseLines.filter((l) => !l.startsWith('\t')).join('\n'),
    ],
    [
      'recipe lines with other content',
      baseLines.map((l) => (l.startsWith('\t') ? '\t@true' : l)).join('\n'),
    ],
    [
      'no dish rules',
      baseLines.filter((l) => !/^(chicken|gravy|greens): /.test(l)).join('\n'),
    ],
    ['no dinner rule', baseLines.filter((l) => !l.startsWith('dinner:')).join('\n')],
    [
      'no space after the colon',
      baseLines.map((l) => l.replace(/^(chicken\.rest): /, '$1:')).join('\n'),
    ],
    [
      'a comment at the end of rule lines',
      baseLines.map((l) => (/^[a-z.]+:( |$)/.test(l) ? `${l} # a note` : l)).join('\n'),
    ],
    [
      'spaces before comment and rule lines',
      baseLines.map((l) => (l.startsWith('#') || /^[a-z.]+:/.test(l) ? `   ${l}` : l)).join('\n'),
    ],
    [
      'blank lines everywhere',
      baseLines.flatMap((l) => [l, '', '   ']).join('\n'),
    ],
    [
      'unknown comments: # Step, # steps and #stepping',
      baseLines.flatMap((l) => [l, '# Step dish=x', '# steps below', '#stepping', '# plan2']).join('\n'),
    ],
    [
      'the step blocks in reverse order',
      [...sections.slice(0, 4), ...sections.slice(4).reverse()].join('\n\n') + '\n',
    ],
    [
      'the plan and dish lines at the bottom',
      [sections[0], ...sections.slice(2), sections[1]].join('\n\n') + '\n',
    ],
    [
      'the dish lines in reverse order',
      baseLines
        .map((l, i) => (i >= 6 && i <= 8 ? (baseLines[14 - i] ?? l) : l))
        .join('\n'),
    ],
    [
      'a repeated prerequisite',
      baseLines.map((l) => l.replace(/^(chicken\.rest): chicken\.roast$/, '$1: chicken.roast chicken.roast')).join('\n'),
    ],
    [
      '\\u0053 for S in the plan title',
      baseLines.map((l) => l.replace('# plan "Sunday roast"', '# plan "\\u0053unday roast"')).join('\n'),
    ],
    [
      'the keys of one step line in another order',
      baseLines
        .map((l) =>
          l.startsWith('# step dish=greens minutes=5')
            ? '# step title="Shred the greens" hold=keeps handsOn=true appliance=none minutes=5 dish=greens'
            : l,
        )
        .join('\n'),
    ],
    [
      'tabs between fields',
      baseLines
        .map((l) => (l.startsWith('# step dish=greens minutes=1') ? l.replaceAll(' ', '\t').replace('#\tstep', '#\tstep') : l))
        .join('\n'),
    ],
  ];

  it.each(variants)('%s gives the same result', (_name, text) => {
    expect(fromDinnerfile(text)).toStrictEqual(SUNDAY_RESULT);
  });

  it('\\/ in a string is /', () => {
    const text = baseLines
      .map((l) => (l.startsWith('# plan ') ? '# plan "Sunday \\/ roast"' : l))
      .join('\n');
    const result = fromDinnerfile(text);
    expect(result.ok && result.value.plan.title).toBe('Sunday / roast');
  });
});

describe('R5.4 # kitchen and # serve', () => {
  const withOptions = (...extra: string[]): string =>
    [...baseLines.slice(0, 6), ...extra, ...baseLines.slice(6)].join('\n');

  it('both lines give both options', () => {
    expect(
      fromDinnerfile(withOptions('# kitchen burners=4 ovens=1 cooks=1', '# serve 19:00')),
    ).toStrictEqual(
      ok({
        plan: canonicalPlan(SUNDAY_ROAST),
        options: { kitchen: { burners: 4, ovens: 1, cooks: 1 }, serve: '19:00' },
      }),
    );
  });
  it('a kitchen alone leaves serve absent; a serving time alone leaves kitchen absent', () => {
    const kitchen = fromDinnerfile(withOptions('# kitchen cooks=2 burners=0 ovens=4'));
    const serve = fromDinnerfile(withOptions('# serve 09:05'));
    expect(kitchen.ok && kitchen.value.options).toStrictEqual({
      kitchen: { burners: 0, ovens: 4, cooks: 2 },
    });
    expect(serve.ok && serve.value.options).toStrictEqual({ serve: '09:05' });
  });
  it('the options come back as the export wrote them', () => {
    const options = { kitchen: HOME_KITCHEN, serve: '19:00' };
    const text = toDinnerfile(SUNDAY_ROAST, options);
    expect(fromDinnerfile(text.ok ? text.value : '')).toStrictEqual(
      ok({ plan: canonicalPlan(SUNDAY_ROAST), options }),
    );
  });
});

describe('R6.4 only the lexical form is checked; ranges are validatePlan\'s', () => {
  it('minutes=0 imports, and validatePlan then reports minutes_out_of_range for that step', () => {
    const text = baseLines
      .map((l) => (l.startsWith('# step dish=chicken minutes=5 ') ? l.replace('minutes=5', 'minutes=0') : l))
      .join('\n');
    const result = fromDinnerfile(text);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    const checked = validatePlan(result.ok ? result.value.plan : null);
    expect(checked.plan).toBeNull();
    expect(checked.issues.map((i) => i.code)).toEqual(['minutes_out_of_range']);
  });
  it('a kitchen of nine ovens imports; parseKitchen is what rejects it', () => {
    const text = [...baseLines.slice(0, 6), '# kitchen burners=4 ovens=9 cooks=1', ...baseLines.slice(6)].join('\n');
    const result = fromDinnerfile(text);
    expect(result.ok && result.value.options.kitchen).toEqual({ burners: 4, ovens: 9, cooks: 1 });
    expect(parseKitchen(result.ok ? result.value.options.kitchen : null).ok).toBe(false);
  });
  it('a six-digit number is lexically fine', () => {
    const text = baseLines
      .map((l) => (l.startsWith('# step dish=chicken minutes=5 ') ? l.replace('minutes=5', 'minutes=999999') : l))
      .join('\n');
    const result = fromDinnerfile(text);
    expect(result.ok && result.value.plan.steps[0]?.minutes).toBe(999999);
  });
});

describe('R8.3 plans with sanity errors still round-trip', () => {
  it('a plan with a cycle comes back as it was, and validatePlan reports the cycle', () => {
    const plan: Plan = {
      title: 'Cycle',
      dishes: [{ id: 'a', name: 'A' }],
      steps: [
        step('a.x', { title: 'X', after: ['a.y'] }),
        step('a.y', { title: 'Y', after: ['a.x'] }),
      ],
    };
    const text = toDinnerfile(plan);
    const back = fromDinnerfile(text.ok ? text.value : '');
    expect(back).toStrictEqual(ok({ plan: canonicalPlan(plan), options: {} }));
    expect(validatePlan(back.ok ? back.value.plan : null).issues.map((i) => i.code)).toEqual([
      'cycle',
    ]);
  });
});

cat >> test/core/dinnerfile.test.ts <<'EOF'

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// T3.2: import errors. One firing example per code with the exact error list, and the
// non-firing neighbours. Small files, so that line numbers are easy to read.

const STEP_X = '# step dish=a minutes=5 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="X"';
const file = (...lines: string[]): string => `${lines.join('\n')}\n`;
/** A good one-step file; `extra` lines go after the rule, so they start at line 5. */
const small = (...extra: string[]): string =>
  file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X, 'a.x:', ...extra);

const errorsOf = (text: string): DinnerfileError[] => {
  const result = fromDinnerfile(text);
  return result.ok ? [] : result.error;
};
const codesOf = (text: string): string[] => errorsOf(text).map((e) => e.code);

const OTHER_MESSAGE =
  'Make Dinner reads only comments, rules like "chicken.carve: chicken.rest" and recipe lines. Remove this line or rewrite it as one of those.';

describe('R7 the good small file imports', () => {
  it('small() is a plan T with dish a and step a.x', () => {
    const result = fromDinnerfile(small());
    expect(result.ok && result.value.plan.steps.map((s) => s.id)).toEqual(['a.x']);
    expect(result.ok && result.value.plan.title).toBe('T');
  });
});

describe('R7.1 unsupported_line', () => {
  it('a variable: reason other', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(small('X = 1'))).toEqual([
      { code: 'unsupported_line', line: 5, reason: 'other', message: `Line 5: ${OTHER_MESSAGE}` },
    ]);
  });
  it.each([
    ['include other', 'a directive'],
    ['ifeq (a,b)', 'a conditional'],
    ['$(X): y', 'a variable target'],
    ['%.o: %.c', 'a pattern rule'],
    ['a.x: $(Y)', 'a variable prerequisite'],
    ['a.x: b.y; echo hi', 'a command after a semicolon'],
    ['a.x: b.y | c.z', 'an order-only prerequisite'],
    ['*.x: b.y', 'a wildcard target'],
    [':', 'a lone colon'],
    [': a.x', 'no target'],
  ])('%s (%s): reason other', (line) => {
    expect(errorsOf(small(line)).map((e) => [e.code, e.line, 'reason' in e && e.reason])).toEqual([
      ['unsupported_line', 5, 'other'],
    ]);
  });
  it('white space before the colon: reason space_before_colon', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(small('a.x : b.y'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'unsupported_line',
        line: 5,
        reason: 'space_before_colon',
        message: 'Line 5: put the ":" directly after the target name.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('two targets: reason several_targets', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(small('a.x b.y:'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'unsupported_line',
        line: 5,
        reason: 'several_targets',
        message: 'Line 5: a rule may have one target. Write one rule per step.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('a double colon: reason double_colon', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(small('a.x:: b.y'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'unsupported_line',
        line: 5,
        reason: 'double_colon',
        message: 'Line 5: use one ":" between the target and its prerequisites.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('non-firing: ifeq: ifeq.x with the colon attached is a rule, ifeq : ifeq.x is not', () => {
    const base = (rule: string): string =>
      file(
        '# plan "T"',
        '# dish ifeq "I"',
        '# step dish=ifeq minutes=1 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="I"',
        'ifeq.x:',
        rule,
      );
    expect(codesOf(base('ifeq: ifeq.x'))).toEqual([]);
    expect(codesOf(base('ifeq : ifeq.x'))).toEqual(['unsupported_line']);
  });
  it('non-firing: a comment, a blank line, a recipe line and a # note after a rule', () => {
    expect(codesOf(small('# any comment', '', '\t@echo hi', 'a.x: # note'))).toEqual([
      'duplicate_target',
    ]);
  });
});

describe('R7.2 line_continuation', () => {
  it('a comment ending in a backslash: one error, the line is read without it', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(file('# plan "T" \\', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X, 'a.x:'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'line_continuation',
        line: 1,
        message:
          'Line 1: the line ends with "\\", which makes Make join it to the next line. Remove the "\\".',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('white space after the backslash does not hide it; a blank line with one is an error too', () => {
    expect(codesOf(small('# note \\  \t'))).toEqual(['line_continuation']);
    expect(codesOf(small(' \\'))).toEqual(['line_continuation']);
  });
  it('a backslash inside a line, not at its end, is fine', () => {
    expect(codesOf(small('# a \\ b'))).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('a recipe line ending in a backslash is an error as well', () => {
    expect(codesOf(small('\t@echo hi \\'))).toEqual(['line_continuation']);
  });
});

describe('R7.3 recipe_before_rule', () => {
  it('a tab line before the first rule', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '\t@true', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X, 'a.x:'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'recipe_before_rule',
        line: 2,
        message:
          'Line 2: this line starts with a tab before any rule, so Make stops here. Remove the tab.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('non-firing: after the first rule a tab line is a recipe, even one that looks like metadata', () => {
    expect(codesOf(small('\t# step dish=x', '\t# plan "Y"', '\t@true'))).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('non-firing: a line of only a tab is blank', () => {
    expect(codesOf(file('\t', '# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X, 'a.x:'))).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('R7.4 bad_target and bad_prerequisite', () => {
  it('a target that is no id: bad_target', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(small('Foo: a.x'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'bad_target',
        line: 5,
        message:
          'Line 5: the target is not a step id (like chicken.roast), a dish id, dinner or .PHONY. Rename it.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it.each(['a_b: a.x', 'a.b.c: a.x', '.hidden: a.x', 'a--b.c.d:'])('%s is a bad target', (rule) => {
    expect(codesOf(small(rule))).toEqual(['bad_target']);
  });
  it('a prerequisite that is no step id: bad_prerequisite, with the step id', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X, 'a.x: B'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'bad_prerequisite',
        line: 4,
        stepId: 'a.x',
        message:
          'Line 4: step a.x lists a prerequisite that is not a step id (like chicken.rest). Only steps can be prerequisites.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('a dish id as a prerequisite is bad too, and a dish rule reports its dish id', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(small('a: a.x a'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'bad_prerequisite',
        line: 5,
        dishId: 'a',
        message:
          'Line 5: dish a lists a prerequisite that is not a step id (like chicken.rest). Only steps can be prerequisites.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('the dinner rule reports it without an id', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(small('dinner: a.x nope'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'bad_prerequisite',
        line: 5,
        message:
          'Line 5: dinner lists a prerequisite that is not a step id (like chicken.rest). Only steps can be prerequisites.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('non-firing: .PHONY may name anything', () => {
    expect(codesOf(small('.PHONY: dinner nowhere Odd_Name'))).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('R7.5 missing_plan and duplicate_metadata', () => {
  it("'' gives exactly missing_plan, with line null", () => {
    expect(errorsOf('')).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'missing_plan',
        line: null,
        message: 'The file has no "# plan" line. Add one near the top, like: # plan "Sunday roast"',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('a file with steps but no # plan line: missing_plan comes first', () => {
    expect(codesOf(file('# dish a "A"', STEP_X, 'a.x:'))).toEqual(['missing_plan']);
  });
  it('a second # plan line', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '# plan "U"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X, 'a.x:'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'duplicate_metadata',
        line: 2,
        keyword: 'plan',
        firstLine: 1,
        message: 'Line 2: a second "# plan" line (the first is line 1). Keep one.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('a second # kitchen line and a second # serve line, each reported at its own line', () => {
    expect(
      errorsOf(
        small(
          '# kitchen burners=1 ovens=1 cooks=1',
          '# kitchen burners=2 ovens=1 cooks=1',
          '# serve 19:00',
          '# serve 20:00',
        ),
      ),
    ).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'duplicate_metadata',
        line: 6,
        keyword: 'kitchen',
        firstLine: 5,
        message: 'Line 6: a second "# kitchen" line (the first is line 5). Keep one.',
      },
      {
        code: 'duplicate_metadata',
        line: 8,
        keyword: 'serve',
        firstLine: 7,
        message: 'Line 8: a second "# serve" line (the first is line 7). Keep one.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('non-firing: several # dish lines are fine', () => {
    expect(codesOf(small('# dish b "B"'))).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('R7.6 metadata lines', () => {
  it.each([
    ['# plan', 'plan', 'a "# plan" line must look like: # plan "Sunday roast"'],
    ['# plan "T" extra', 'plan', 'a "# plan" line must look like: # plan "Sunday roast"'],
    ['# dish a', 'dish', 'a "# dish" line must look like: # dish gravy "Gravy"'],
    ['# dish', 'dish', 'a "# dish" line must look like: # dish gravy "Gravy"'],
    ['# dish a "A" extra', 'dish', 'a "# dish" line must look like: # dish gravy "Gravy"'],
    ['# serve', 'serve', 'a "# serve" line must look like: # serve 19:00'],
    ['# serve 19:00 20:00', 'serve', 'a "# serve" line must look like: # serve 19:00'],
    [
      '# step dish',
      'step',
      'a "# step" line must be a list of key=value fields, like: # step dish=gravy minutes=2 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="Strain the gravy"',
    ],
    [
      '# kitchen 4',
      'kitchen',
      'a "# kitchen" line must be a list of key=value fields, like: # kitchen burners=4 ovens=1 cooks=1',
    ],
  ])('%s is bad_metadata (%s)', (line, keyword, shape) => {
    const text = line.startsWith('# plan')
      ? file(line, '# dish a "A"', STEP_X, 'a.x:')
      : line.startsWith('# step')
        ? file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', line, 'a.x:')
        : small(line);
    const errors = errorsOf(text);
    const at = line.startsWith('# plan') ? 1 : line.startsWith('# step') ? 3 : 5;
    expect(errors).toEqual([
      { code: 'bad_metadata', line: at, keyword, message: `Line ${at}: ${shape}` },
    ]);
  });
  it('an unknown key in a # step line: unknown_key with the step id', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', `${STEP_X} extra=1`, 'a.x:'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'unknown_key',
        line: 3,
        keyword: 'step',
        stepId: 'a.x',
        message:
          'Line 3: the "# step" line has a key this app does not use. The keys are dish, minutes, appliance, handsOn, hold, title and detail.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('an unknown key in a # kitchen line names the kitchen keys', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(small('# kitchen burners=1 ovens=1 cooks=1 sinks=2'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'unknown_key',
        line: 5,
        keyword: 'kitchen',
        message:
          'Line 5: the "# kitchen" line has a key this app does not use. The keys are burners, ovens and cooks.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('a key twice: duplicate_key, naming the key', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', `${STEP_X} minutes=6`, 'a.x:'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'duplicate_key',
        line: 3,
        keyword: 'step',
        key: 'minutes',
        stepId: 'a.x',
        message: 'Line 3: the "# step" line gives minutes twice. Keep one.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('a required key missing: missing_key, naming the key', () => {
    const noHold = STEP_X.replace(' hold=keeps', '');
    expect(errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', noHold, 'a.x:'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'missing_key',
        line: 3,
        keyword: 'step',
        key: 'hold',
        stepId: 'a.x',
        message: 'Line 3: the "# step" line has no hold. Add hold=<minutes> or hold=keeps.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('a kitchen key missing', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(small('# kitchen burners=1 ovens=1'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'missing_key',
        line: 5,
        keyword: 'kitchen',
        key: 'cooks',
        message: 'Line 5: the "# kitchen" line has no cooks. Add cooks=<number>.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('non-firing: detail is optional, and the keys may come in any order', () => {
    expect(
      codesOf(
        file(
          '# plan "T"',
          '# dish a "A"',
          '# step title="X" detail="More" hold=3 handsOn=false appliance=oven minutes=5 dish=a',
          'a.x:',
        ),
      ),
    ).toEqual([]);
  });

  describe('bad_value, one fixed sentence per key', () => {
    const stepWith = (from: string, to: string): string =>
      file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X.replace(from, to), 'a.x:');
    it.each([
      ['minutes=5', 'minutes=1.5', 'minutes', 'minutes must be a whole number with no sign or leading zero, like minutes=15.'],
      ['minutes=5', 'minutes=-3', 'minutes', 'minutes must be a whole number with no sign or leading zero, like minutes=15.'],
      ['minutes=5', 'minutes=007', 'minutes', 'minutes must be a whole number with no sign or leading zero, like minutes=15.'],
      ['minutes=5', 'minutes=', 'minutes', 'minutes must be a whole number with no sign or leading zero, like minutes=15.'],
      ['minutes=5', 'minutes=abc', 'minutes', 'minutes must be a whole number with no sign or leading zero, like minutes=15.'],
      ['minutes=5', 'minutes=1e3', 'minutes', 'minutes must be a whole number with no sign or leading zero, like minutes=15.'],
      ['minutes=5', 'minutes=1234567', 'minutes', 'minutes must be a whole number with no sign or leading zero, like minutes=15.'],
      ['minutes=5', 'minutes="5"', 'minutes', 'minutes must be a whole number with no sign or leading zero, like minutes=15.'],
      ['hold=keeps', 'hold=keep', 'hold', 'hold must be a whole number with no sign or leading zero, or keeps, like hold=5.'],
      ['hold=keeps', 'hold=-1', 'hold', 'hold must be a whole number with no sign or leading zero, or keeps, like hold=5.'],
      ['appliance=none', 'appliance=stove', 'appliance', 'appliance must be burner, oven or none.'],
      ['handsOn=true', 'handsOn=yes', 'handsOn', 'handsOn must be true or false.'],
      ['handsOn=true', 'handsOn=True', 'handsOn', 'handsOn must be true or false.'],
      ['dish=a', 'dish=A', 'dish', 'dish must be a dish id like chicken: lowercase letters and digits, with single hyphens between them.'],
      ['dish=a', 'dish=a--b', 'dish', 'dish must be a dish id like chicken: lowercase letters and digits, with single hyphens between them.'],
      ['title="X"', 'title=X', 'title', 'title must be text in double quotes, like title="Carve the chicken".'],
    ])('%s -> %s', (from, to, key, sentence) => {
      expect(errorsOf(stepWith(from, to))).toEqual([
        { code: 'bad_value', line: 3, keyword: 'step', key, stepId: 'a.x', message: `Line 3: ${sentence}` },
      ]);
    });
    it('a detail that is not a string', () => {
      expect(errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', `${STEP_X} detail=more`, 'a.x:'))).toEqual([
        {
          code: 'bad_value',
          line: 3,
          keyword: 'step',
          key: 'detail',
          stepId: 'a.x',
          message: 'Line 3: detail must be text in double quotes, like detail="Use the tin juices".',
        },
      ]);
    });
    it('an unterminated string and a bad escape in a title', () => {
      expect(codesOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', `${STEP_X.replace('title="X"', 'title="X')}`, 'a.x:'))).toEqual(['bad_value']);
      expect(codesOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X.replace('title="X"', 'title="X\\q"'), 'a.x:'))).toEqual(['bad_value']);
      expect(codesOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X.replace('title="X"', 'title="X\\u12"'), 'a.x:'))).toEqual(['bad_value']);
    });
    it('a kitchen count and a serving time', () => {
      expect(errorsOf(small('# kitchen burners=-1 ovens=1 cooks=1'))).toEqual([
        {
          code: 'bad_value',
          line: 5,
          keyword: 'kitchen',
          key: 'burners',
          message: 'Line 5: burners must be a whole number with no sign or leading zero, like burners=4.',
        },
      ]);
      expect(errorsOf(small('# serve 25:00'))).toEqual([
        {
          code: 'bad_value',
          line: 5,
          keyword: 'serve',
          key: 'serve',
          message: 'Line 5: the serving time must be a 24-hour time like 19:00.',
        },
      ]);
    });
    it('a plan title that is not quoted, and a dish line with a bad id or an unquoted name', () => {
      expect(errorsOf(file('# plan T', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X, 'a.x:'))).toEqual([
        {
          code: 'bad_value',
          line: 1,
          keyword: 'plan',
          key: 'title',
          message: 'Line 1: the plan title must be text in double quotes, like "Sunday roast".',
        },
      ]);
      expect(errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish A "A"', STEP_X, 'a.x:'))).toEqual([
        {
          code: 'bad_value',
          line: 2,
          keyword: 'dish',
          key: 'dish',
          message:
            'Line 2: the dish id must be lowercase letters and digits with single hyphens, like gravy.',
        },
      ]);
      expect(errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a A', STEP_X, 'a.x:'))).toEqual([
        {
          code: 'bad_value',
          line: 2,
          keyword: 'dish',
          key: 'name',
          message: 'Line 2: the dish name must be text in double quotes, like "Gravy".',
        },
      ]);
    });
    it('non-firing: minutes=0 and hold=0 are lexically fine, as are a 6-digit number and \\u escapes', () => {
      const ok0 = file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X.replace('minutes=5', 'minutes=0').replace('hold=keeps', 'hold=0'), 'a.x:');
      expect(codesOf(ok0)).toEqual([]);
      expect(codesOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X.replace('minutes=5', 'minutes=999999').replace('title="X"', 'title="\\u0058\\/"'), 'a.x:'))).toEqual([]);
    });
  });
});

describe('R7.7 step_without_target and target_without_step', () => {
  it('a # step line followed by a blank line and the next # step line', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X, '', STEP_X.replace('"X"', '"Y"'), 'a.y:'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'step_without_target',
        line: 3,
        message:
          'Line 3: this "# step" line is not followed by a step rule. Put it directly above the rule of its step, or remove it.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('a # step line at the end of the text', () => {
    expect(codesOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X))).toEqual(['step_without_target']);
  });
  it('a # step line followed by a rule that is not a step rule (a dish rule)', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X, 'a: a.x'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'step_without_target',
        line: 3,
        message:
          'Line 3: this "# step" line is not followed by a step rule. Put it directly above the rule of its step, or remove it.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('a step rule with no # step line', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', 'greens.toss: greens.boil'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'target_without_step',
        line: 3,
        stepId: 'greens.toss',
        message:
          'Line 3: step greens.toss has no "# step" line above it. Add one, like: # step dish=greens minutes=1 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="Describe the step"',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('non-firing: blank lines, comments and other metadata between the # step line and its rule', () => {
    expect(
      codesOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X, '', '# a note', '# dish b "B"', '   ', 'a.x:')),
    ).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('a # step line with a bad value still attaches: no target_without_step, and its errors carry the step id', () => {
    const errors = errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X.replace('minutes=5', 'minutes=x'), 'a.x:'));
    expect(errors.map((e) => e.code)).toEqual(['bad_value']);
    expect(errors[0] && 'stepId' in errors[0] && errors[0].stepId).toBe('a.x');
  });
  it('a # step line that attaches to nothing keeps its errors without a step id', () => {
    const errors = errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X.replace('minutes=5', 'minutes=x')));
    expect(errors.map((e) => [e.code, 'stepId' in e ? e.stepId : 'none'])).toEqual([
      ['bad_value', 'none'],
      ['step_without_target', 'none'],
    ].map(([c, s]) => [c, s]));
  });
});

describe('R7.8 duplicate_target', () => {
  it('a step rule twice: the later one is reported, with its step id and the first line', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X, 'a.x:', STEP_X, 'a.x: a.y'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'duplicate_target',
        line: 6,
        target: 'a.x',
        firstLine: 4,
        stepId: 'a.x',
        message:
          'Line 6: a.x is the target of a second rule (the first is line 4). Make would merge their prerequisites; keep one rule.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('a dish rule twice and the dinner rule twice', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(small('a: a.x', 'a: a.x', 'dinner: a.x', 'dinner: a.x'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'duplicate_target',
        line: 7,
        target: 'a',
        firstLine: 6,
        dishId: 'a',
        message:
          'Line 7: a is the target of a second rule (the first is line 6). Make would merge their prerequisites; keep one rule.',
      },
      {
        code: 'duplicate_target',
        line: 9,
        target: 'dinner',
        firstLine: 8,
        message:
          'Line 9: dinner is the target of a second rule (the first is line 8). Make would merge their prerequisites; keep one rule.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('non-firing: .PHONY twice is fine, and a dish rule and a step rule never collide', () => {
    expect(codesOf(small('.PHONY: a', '.PHONY: a.x', 'a: a.x', 'dinner: a.x'))).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('R7.9 dish_target_mismatch and dinner_mismatch', () => {
  const two = (...rules: string[]): string =>
    file(
      '# plan "T"',
      '# dish a "A"',
      STEP_X,
      'a.x:',
      '',
      STEP_X.replace('"X"', '"Y"'),
      'a.y: a.x',
      ...rules,
    );

  it('a dish rule that lists a step that is not a last step of the dish', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(two('a: a.x a.y'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'dish_target_mismatch',
        line: 8,
        dishId: 'a',
        message: "Line 8: the rule for dish a must list the dish's last steps: a.y. Fix it or remove the rule.",
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('a dish rule that lists too few steps (here: none)', () => {
    expect(codesOf(two('a:'))).toEqual(['dish_target_mismatch']);
  });
  it('a dish rule for a dish with no # dish line', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(two('sauce: a.y'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'dish_target_mismatch',
        line: 8,
        dishId: 'sauce',
        message: 'Line 8: there is no "# dish sauce" line for the rule sauce. Add the dish or remove the rule.',
      },
    ]);
  });
  it('a dinner rule that lists a step that is not terminal, or misses one', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(two('dinner: a.x a.y'))).toEqual([
      {
        code: 'dinner_mismatch',
        line: 8,
        message: "Line 8: dinner must list the plan's terminal steps: a.y. Fix it or remove the rule.",
      },
    ]);
    expect(codesOf(two('dinner:'))).toEqual(['dinner_mismatch']);
  });
  it('non-firing: the right sets, in any order and with repeats, and no rule at all', () => {
    expect(codesOf(two('a: a.y a.y', 'dinner: a.y'))).toEqual([]);
    expect(codesOf(two())).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('R7.13 no mismatch is reported when another error was found', () => {
    expect(codesOf(two('a: a.x a.y', 'dinner:', 'X = 1'))).toEqual(['unsupported_line']);
  });
});

describe('R7.12 several errors, order, duplicates and the cap', () => {
  it('errors come back in line order, then by code', () => {
    const text = file(
      '# plan "T"',
      '# dish a "A"',
      STEP_X.replace('minutes=5', 'minutes=x'),
      'a.x:',
      'X = 1',
      'Foo: a.x',
      '# serve 25:00',
    );
    expect(errorsOf(text).map((e) => [e.line, e.code])).toEqual([
      [3, 'bad_value'],
      [5, 'unsupported_line'],
      [6, 'bad_target'],
      [7, 'bad_value'],
    ]);
  });
  it('missing_plan has line null and comes first', () => {
    expect(errorsOf(file('X = 1')).map((e) => [e.line, e.code])).toEqual([
      [null, 'missing_plan'],
      [1, 'unsupported_line'],
    ]);
  });
  it('two bad values on one line are two errors; the same unknown key twice is one', () => {
    expect(
      errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', STEP_X.replace('minutes=5', 'minutes=x').replace('hold=keeps', 'hold=y'), 'a.x:')).map((e) => 'key' in e && e.key),
    ).toEqual(['hold', 'minutes']);
    expect(codesOf(file('# plan "T"', '# dish a "A"', `${STEP_X} q=1 r=2`, 'a.x:'))).toEqual(['unknown_key']);
  });
  it('R7.12 at most 50 errors, then one more entry that says how many were left out', () => {
    const lines = Array.from({ length: 60 }, () => 'X = 1');
    const errors = errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', ...lines));
    expect(errors.length).toBe(51);
    expect(errors.slice(0, 50).every((e) => e.code === 'unsupported_line')).toBe(true);
    expect(errors.slice(0, 50).map((e) => e.line)).toEqual(Array.from({ length: 50 }, (_, i) => i + 2));
    expect(errors[50]).toEqual({
      code: 'too_many_errors',
      line: 51,
      count: 10,
      message: 'Line 51: 10 more problems were found after this point. Fix the problems above and import again.',
    });
  });
  it('exactly 50 errors are all shown, with no summary', () => {
    const lines = Array.from({ length: 50 }, () => 'X = 1');
    const errors = errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', ...lines));
    expect(errors.length).toBe(50);
    expect(errors.every((e) => e.code === 'unsupported_line')).toBe(true);
  });
  it('51 errors: 50 and a summary of 1', () => {
    const errors = errorsOf(file('# plan "T"', ...Array.from({ length: 51 }, () => 'X = 1')));
    expect(errors.length).toBe(51);
    expect(errors[50]).toMatchObject({ code: 'too_many_errors', count: 1 });
  });
  it('R7.11 messages never hold text from the file: a hostile key, id-like word and title stay out', () => {
    const hostile = file(
      '# plan "T"',
      '# dish a "A"',
      '# step dish=a minutes=5 appliance=none handsOn=true hold=keeps title="X" $(evil)=1 touch-canary=2',
      'a.x: $(evil) touch-canary',
      'rm -rf canary',
      '`touch canary`: x',
    );
    const text = errorsOf(hostile).map((e) => e.message).join('\n');
    expect(text.includes('evil')).toBe(false);
    expect(text.includes('canary')).toBe(false);
    expect(/^[\x20-\x7e\n]+$/.test(text)).toBe(true);
  });
});

/** Both sizes of plan, for properties that take a plan and then something drawn from it. */
const forPlansThen = <T>(
  then: (plan: Plan) => fc.Arbitrary<T>,
  check: (plan: Plan, drawn: T) => void,
): void => {
  for (const large of [false, true]) {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        arbPlan(large ? { large: true } : {}).chain((plan) =>
          then(plan).map((drawn) => ({ plan, drawn })),
        ),
        ({ plan, drawn }) => {

        },
      ),
      fcParams(),

/** `a` before `b` in the order of R7.12: line (null first), code, step id, key. */
const compareErrors = (a: DinnerfileError, b: DinnerfileError): number => {
  const part = (e: DinnerfileError): [number, string, string, string] => [
    e.line === null ? -1 : e.line,
    e.code,
    'stepId' in e && e.stepId !== undefined ? e.stepId : '',
    'key' in e ? e.key : '',
  ];
  const [la, ca, sa, ka] = part(a);
  const [lb, cb, sb, kb] = part(b);
  if (la !== lb) return la - lb;
  for (const [x, y] of [
    [ca, cb],
    [sa, sb],
    [ka, kb],
  ] as const) {
    if (x < y) return -1;
    if (x > y) return 1;
  }
  return 0;
};

it(
  'P-DF1 total: fromDinnerfile never throws, gives a plan or at least one well-formed error, sorted and without repeats',
  () => {
    const check = (text: string): void => {
      const result = fromDinnerfile(text);
      if (result.ok) {
        expect(result.value.plan).toStrictEqual(canonicalPlan(result.value.plan));
        return;
      }
